Motivation: 自动识别羊的疼痛表情有助于持续福利评估，但实际应用要求模型输出具有可信、可验证的临床依据。单纯展示注意力图、门控权重或生成文本并不能证明模型真正使用了相应的疼痛表情指标，因此需要检验解释的忠实性，并构建能够受临床概念约束、且可由区域级标注验证的模型。

Method: 首先将检测到的面部区域与SPFES临床描述的文本嵌入进行注意力交互，并通过描述消融、注意力与预测一致性等方式评估解释是否具有语义意义。随后移除外观信息旁路，构建概念瓶颈模型，使分类器只能读取SPFES概念分数；模型使用每个面部区域的状态标注进行监督。研究还比较了有无区域级概念监督的设置，分析类别不均衡对概念准确率的影响，并在统一协议和交叉验证下比较七种方法。

Result: 初始注意力式解释缺乏忠实性：删除一个完整描述仅使预测logit变化约10^-4，最受关注的线索与预测疼痛等级在区域层面仅有32.6%一致，尽管注意力图、学习到的门控和生成文本都给出了相反暗示。概念瓶颈模型使Cohen's κ下降约0.05--0.10，但少数疼痛指示状态的恢复率达到其基线比例的3.5--8.3倍，并且在没有严重度监督时仍能形成耳部和眼部的严重度排序。去除概念监督后，κ基本不变，但概念准确率降至0.109，说明模型结构上的概念通道并不等于真正学习了有语义意义的概念。研究还表明，受临床类别不均衡影响，汇总概念准确率会掩盖少数关键状态的表现。

Conclusion: 可信解释不能由注意力或生成文本的表面表现直接推断，必须通过干预实验和概念级标注验证。对于羊疼痛识别，概念瓶颈结构结合区域级监督能够提升解释的语义有效性和可审计性，但需要接受一定预测性能损失。临床不均衡场景下，应采用与任务协议匹配、关注少数状态恢复能力的评估指标，而不能只依赖总体准确率或传统分类性能。

Abstract: Automated pain recognition from facial expression could make continuous welfare assessment practical in sheep, but adoption depends on trust: a stockperson cannot act on a score that arrives without justification. We ground a model in the Sheep Pain Facial Expression Scale (SPFES) by letting each detected facial region attend over text embeddings of the clinical descriptors and then test whether the resulting explanations mean anything. They do not. Ablating an entire descriptor changes the predicted logit by about $10^{-4}$, and the most-attended cue agrees with the predicted pain level in only $32.6\%$ of regions, although the attention maps, the learned gate, and the generated text all proposed otherwise. We therefore remove the appearance bypass with a concept bottleneck whose classifier reads only SPFES concept scores, supervised by per-region state annotations that image-level pipelines discard. This costs $0.05$--$0.10$ in Cohen's $κ$ but yields concepts that are demonstrably learned: minority pain-indicating states are recovered at $3.5$--$8.3\times$ their base rates, and the ear and eye severity orderings emerge without severity supervision. Removing the supervision alone leaves $κ$ unchanged while concept accuracy falls to $0.109$, showing that architectural necessity does not imply semantic validity. We also show that pooled concept accuracy is misleading under clinical imbalance and provide a cross-validated, protocol-matched benchmark of seven methods on this dataset.

</details>


### [64] [Cross-Architecture Foundation-Model Distillation for Edge Flood Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20441)
*Fabian Schmalstieg,Karsten Mueller,Wojciech Samek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究将大型地理空间基础模型Prithvi-EO-2.0蒸馏为极小型EfficientViT-B0，用于洪水分割边缘部署。通过教师模型监督未标注Sentinel-2影像，在不增加人工标注的情况下扩大训练数据。结果表明，扩展到2,500个场景后，学生模型在部分测试集接近或达到教师模型，同时经量化后可在Jetson Xavier NX上以低延迟运行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地理空间基础模型具有较强的洪水分割能力，但参数规模和运行内存较大，难以部署到资源受限的边缘设备。研究的核心动机是：在固定人工标注预算下，利用基础模型产生的监督信号扩大有效训练集，并训练出体积小、推理快、适合边缘部署的洪水分割模型。

Method: 以300M参数的Prithvi-EO-2.0作为教师模型，并在252个Sen1Floods11人工标注训练场景上进行微调；将其知识蒸馏到仅约0.7M参数的EfficientViT-B0学生模型。教师模型进一步监督未标注的Sentinel-2影像，以构建更大规模的伪标注或教师监督训练池，并比较252场景与2,500场景配置。实验包含几何匹配控制，以区分数据几何分布与标签来源的影响；同时进行激活替换和量化感知训练，生成INT8 TensorRT边缘推理引擎。研究还将模型与固定MNDWI阈值规则进行比较。

Result: 在252个场景的匹配训练预算下，教师监督训练与直接训练相比具有竞争力，并在STURM-Flood上改善性能。扩展至2,500个场景后，浮点学生模型在Sen1Floods11测试集上的水体IoU达到0.787，而教师为0.822；在作者的评估协议下，学生在STURM-Flood上达到与教师相当的表现，但在WorldFloods-v2上仍低于教师。量化后的学生模型大小约为1.5MB，以INT8 TensorRT引擎运行，在Jetson Xavier NX上对512×512图像的GPU计算时间为5.57ms，运行时设备内存约14MB。固定MNDWI阈值在两个干净的外部基准上与学习模型具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 基础模型监督能够将有限的人工标注预算转化为更大规模的有效训练数据，并显著缩小轻量学生模型与大型教师模型之间的性能差距。该方法最终得到一个极小、低延迟、低内存占用的边缘洪水分割模型。不过，外部基准上的MNDWI结果说明，学习模型相对简单光谱规则的优势并非普遍成立，因此这些数据集更适合被视为泛化测试，而非单纯证明模型优越性的依据。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models can provide strong flood-segmentation performance, but their size limits deployment on memory-constrained edge hardware. We distill a 300-million-parameter Prithvi-EO-2.0 teacher, fine-tuned on the 252 manually labeled Sen1Floods11 training scenes, into a 0.7-million-parameter EfficientViT-B0 student. The teacher supervises additional unlabeled Sentinel-2 imagery, allowing the student training set to grow without new manual annotations. At the matched budget of 252 scenes, teacher-supervised training is competitive with direct training and improves STURM-Flood performance across tested configurations; a geometry-matched control shows that label source alone does not explain the difference. Scaling the teacher-supervised pool to 2,500 scenes narrows the remaining student--teacher gap: the float student reaches 0.787 water intersection over union on the Sen1Floods11 test split against 0.822 for the teacher, matches the teacher on STURM-Flood under our evaluation protocol, and remains below it on WorldFloods-v2. After activation replacement and quantization-aware training, the student runs as a 1.5-megabyte 8-bit integer (INT8) TensorRT engine on a Jetson Xavier NX at 5.57 milliseconds of graphics processing unit (GPU) compute per 512-by-512 image, with approximately 14 megabytes of runtime device memory. A fixed modified normalized difference water index (MNDWI) threshold is competitive with both models on the two clean external benchmarks, so we interpret those benchmarks as generalization tests rather than as evidence of learned-model superiority over a spectral rule. The results support the conclusion: foundation-model supervision can amplify a fixed manual annotation budget into a substantially larger training set and yield a compact, deployable edge model.

</details>


### [65] [SenseFuse: Label-Free Fusion of Image and Shape Encoders for Open-Vocabulary 3D Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20475)
*Euiseok Han,Tri Ton,Hwanhee Kim,Seungyeon Ryu,Chang D. Yoo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseFuse 是一种无需标签的自适应融合方法，用于开放词汇三维实例分割。它在已有流程的掩码标注阶段融合二维图像编码器与三维形状编码器，并依据单场景无标注候选掩码估计敏感性，自动选择场景级融合权重。该方法在 ScanNet200、Replica 和 ScanNet++ 上普遍提升标注准确率，在 22 个设置中的 21 个提升实例 AP，并取得理想权重收益的中位数 93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇三维实例分割方法在掩码标注阶段主要依赖二维图像特征或由图像蒸馏得到的表示，未充分利用三维形状信息。相比之下，闭集方法已证明三维几何信息的重要性。论文观察到二维图像编码器与三维形状编码器的错误模式大多不重合，而多个二维编码器往往重复相同错误，因此二者具有互补性。核心动机是利用这种互补性，在无需额外标注和重新训练的情况下提升开放词汇三维实例的类别标注质量。

Method: 论文提出 SenseFuse，一种仅改进现有流程掩码标注阶段的无标签融合方法。方法对二维图像编码器和三维形状编码器的输出进行融合，并自适应地为每个场景选择融合权重。该权重通过单个场景中无标签候选掩码计算的敏感性指标进行估计，整个过程在毫秒级完成，因此无需训练标签、人工调参或额外监督。

Result: 在 ScanNet200、Replica 和 ScanNet++ 上，SenseFuse 在所有评估设置中都提升了标注准确率；相对于使用预知最优权重的理想情况，它恢复了 67%–100% 的可获得增益，中位数为 93%。在 22 个报告设置中，有 21 个设置的实例 AP 得到提升，说明二维与三维特征融合具有稳定的实际收益。

Conclusion: 该摘要表明，二维视觉语义与三维形状信息在开放词汇三维实例分割中具有显著互补性。SenseFuse 通过场景级、无标签的自适应权重选择，有效弥补了现有方法过度依赖二维特征的不足，并能以较低计算开销直接增强已有分割管线。其主要贡献在于验证了跨模态错误互补性，并将这种互补性转化为无需训练的实用融合机制。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary scene understanding is fundamental for robotics, laying the groundwork for spatial reasoning and object manipulation. While closed-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation heavily leverages 3D shape information, state-of-the-art open-vocabulary methods remain predominantly restricted to 2D image features or image-distilled representations during mask labeling. In this paper, we propose SenseFuse, a label-free fusion method that balances 2D image and 3D shape encoders for robust open-vocabulary 3D instance segmentation, refining only the mask-labeling stage of existing pipelines. We reveal that 2D image and 3D shape encoders exhibit largely disjoint failure patterns and rarely share identical wrong labels, whereas two 2D image encoders frequently repeat the same errors. This distinct behavior makes the 2D and 3D pair inherently complementary. We introduce an adaptive mechanism that selects a scene-level fusion weight to maximize a label-free sensitivity measure, estimated directly from a single scene's unlabeled proposals in milliseconds. SenseFuse improves labeling accuracy in every evaluated setting across ScanNet200, Replica, and ScanNet++, recovering 67-100% (median 93%) of the gain achievable with an oracle weight, and it raises instance AP in 21 of 22 reported settings. Code is available at https://github.com/hanes1207/SenseFuse.

</details>


### [66] [Grounded Product Understanding in Livestream Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20508)
*Xinyu Zhang,Junjie Chen,Jiawei Ge,Qianlong Li,Libin Ma,Baokun Pan,Yahui Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GPUB基准和UniPro模型，面向电商直播中的联合商品识别与证据时刻定位。GPUB包含3,000个直播实例、31K以上时尚商品，并定义GPrU主任务及商品检索、商品片段定位两个子任务。现有多模态模型表现较弱，最佳基线Pair mAP@0.3仅10.13%；UniPro将性能提升至21.53%，Joint R@1@0.3达到37.23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商直播中的商品信息通常分散在多个时间片段，商品身份与其相关视频证据之间存在关键对应关系。现有商品理解基准通常将商品检索和时间定位分开评估，无法衡量模型是否找到了正确商品及其对应证据，因此需要一个联合评测基准和相应模型。

Method: 构建GPUB大规模基准，包含3,000个经过质量控制并带有多时刻时间标注的直播实例，以及超过31K件时尚商品目录。基准定义三个任务：联合商品理解（GPrU）、商品检索和商品时刻定位。进一步提出UniPro，通过共享多模态编码学习商品对齐且具有时间结构的表示，以统一完成商品识别和相关时刻定位。

Result: 现有多模态模型在GPrU上表现困难，最佳基线Pair mAP@0.3为10.13%。UniPro将Pair mAP@0.3提升至21.53%，并取得37.23%的Joint R@1@0.3。结果表明，联合建模商品身份与时间证据能够显著改善性能，但任务仍具有较大挑战。

Conclusion: 本文通过GPUB填补了商品身份—时间证据对应关系评测的空白，并以UniPro验证了统一商品理解建模的有效性。研究表明，电商直播商品理解需要同时处理跨时刻证据聚合、商品检索和时间定位，而非将这些能力孤立评估。

Abstract: E-commerce livestreams have emerged as an important channel for presenting products to online consumers, containing multiple products whose information is scattered in different moments. This poses significant challenges for downstream product understanding applications, such as product-centric livestream clipping, where models need to identify the product and its relevant segments for information gathering. However, existing benchmarks for general product understanding typically evaluate product retrieval and temporal localization in isolation, leaving the critical correspondence between product identity and temporal evidence largely unassessed. To address this limitation, we introduce GPUB, a large-scale benchmark comprising 3,000 livestream instances with quality-controlled multi-moment temporal annotations and a catalog of over 31K fashion products. GPUB supports three evaluation tasks: the main task Grounded Product Understanding (GPrU) requires jointly identifying the target product and localizing its supporting moments from a livestream video and a candidate product set; Product Retrieval and Product Moment Localization serve as two complementary subtasks. Evaluation of existing multimodal models shows that GPrU remains highly challenging, with the best-performing baseline achieving only 10.13% Pair mAP@.3. To narrow the performance gap, we further develop UniPro, a unified product understanding model that derives product-aligned and temporally structured representations from shared multimodal encoding, improving Pair mAP@.3 to 21.53% while achieving 37.23% Joint R@1@.3 on GPrU.

</details>


### [67] [Automated Goldsmith's Mark Retrieval in Silverware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20509)
*Atmik Tiwari,Vincent Christlein,Mark Fichtner,Freya Gohlke,Birgit Schübel,Theresa Witting,Heike Zech,Mathias Zinnen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个用于金匠标记检索的AI辅助流程，将标记定位、图像裁剪与度量学习结合，并比较ResNet-50、监督式ViT-S/16和自监督DINOv2 ViT-S/14三种骨干网络。最佳配置为DINOv2配合人工裁剪和度量学习微调，mAP达到62.63%，Top-1准确率达到73.74%。结果表明，自监督预训练与标记定位最为关键，学习式裁剪在无需推理阶段人工标注的情况下可获得接近人工裁剪的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金匠标记对于艺术品的身份识别和年代判断具有重要价值，但传统方法依赖专家将查询标记与大量文档化样本进行人工比对，过程耗时且高度依赖专业知识。因此，研究旨在构建自动化、可复现且便于数字人文领域采用的金匠标记检索系统。

Method: 构建包含标记定位与检索的AI流程，并对三种视觉骨干网络进行系统评估：ImageNet预训练ResNet-50、监督式ViT-S/16和自监督DINOv2 ViT-S/14。采用度量学习进行微调，比较不裁剪、人工真值裁剪和基于学习的检测裁剪三种策略及其与不同骨干网络的交互效果。同时发布人工标注数据集和代码，并通过公共网页界面部署系统。

Result: 最佳方案为DINOv2 ViT-S/14、人工裁剪与度量学习微调的组合，取得62.63%的mAP和73.74%的Top-1准确率。实验显示，自监督预训练和金匠标记定位是性能提升最显著的两个因素；学习式裁剪能够恢复人工裁剪带来的大部分性能增益，并且在推理阶段不需要真值标注。

Conclusion: 金匠标记检索性能同时受表征学习方式和目标区域定位质量显著影响。自监督视觉预训练结合有效的标记裁剪是最具潜力的方案，而检测式裁剪提高了系统的实际可用性。通过开放数据、代码和在线系统，本文增强了研究结果的可复现性及其在数字人文中的应用价值。

Abstract: For art historians, goldsmith marks play a critical role in the identification and dating of artifacts. In practice, experts must manually compare a query mark against hundreds of documented examples, a process that is both tedious and highly dependent on specialist knowledge. To address this, we present an AI-assisted retrieval pipeline that combines mark localization with metric-learning fine-tuning across three backbone architectures: an ImageNet-pretrained ResNet-50, a supervised ViT-S/16, and a self-supervised DINOv2 ViT-S/14. We conduct a systematic evaluation of cropping strategies, where we measure the impact of no cropping, manual ground-truth cropping, and learned detection-based cropping, and assess their interaction with each backbone. Our strongest configuration, DINOv2 ViT-S/14 with manual crop and metric-learning fine-tuning, achieves an mAP of 62.63% and a Top-1 accuracy of 73.74%. Our experiments show that self-supervised pretraining and mark localization are the two most impactful factors, with learned cropping recovering the majority of the gain from manual cropping without requiring ground-truth annotations at inference time. To enable reproducibility and adoption in the digital humanities, we release our manually annotated dataset and codebase, and deploy the system via a public web interface.

</details>


### [68] [A Dual-Stream Regulated Reconstruction and Segmentation Network with Hierarchical Artifact-Prior Modeling for Ultra-Low-Field Pediatric Neuroimaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20562)
*Bahram Jafrasteh,Leo Milecki,Qingyu Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一个统一推理框架，同时完成0.064 T超低场儿科MRI的图像质量评估、图像增强和多结构分割。框架以双流3D U-Net为核心，先重建增强体积，再融合原始与增强图像进行分割，并结合伪影预测、标签传播和解剖先验提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 0.064 T超低场儿科MRI存在信噪比低、解剖边界模糊和伪影频发等问题，使图像增强、质量评估和多结构分割均较困难。此外，精细密集标注稀缺，限制了监督学习方法的训练。现有方法通常将三项任务分开处理，难以利用任务间的互补信息，因此需要一个联合且数据高效的解决方案。

Method: 提出基于3D U-Net的双流耦合网络：第一阶段重建增强的超低场MRI体积，第二阶段将原始图像与增强图像联合输入，用于皮下结构分割。为提高边界稳定性，引入一个表示目标结构之外脑组织的辅助类别，该类别由全脑掩膜生成。质量评估分支以重建残差和冻结的分割特征为输入，并通过伪影图条件化的预测头估计七项伪影评分。针对密集标注不足，采用从图谱到目标图像的微分同胚配准进行标签传播，同时利用配准结果约束解剖重建。

Result: 作者报告了该框架在LISA 2026 Challenge三个任务上的验证结果，覆盖图像增强、多个结构分割和七项伪影质量评估。摘要未提供具体数值、基线比较或各任务的详细性能，因此无法据此判断相对改进幅度。

Conclusion: 该研究构建了一个统一的多任务推理流程，将增强、分割和质量评估整合到同一框架中，并结合辅助脑组织类别、伪影图条件化预测、图谱标签传播和解剖配准正则化，以应对超低场儿科MRI中的低信噪比、边界不清、伪影和标注稀缺问题。总体上，方法强调多任务协同和解剖先验，但其有效性仍需结合完整实验数据、消融实验及独立测试结果进一步验证。

Abstract: Automated quality assessment, enhancement, and segmentation of multiple structures in $0.064\,\mathrm{T}$ ultra-low-field pediatric MRI are limited by a low signal-to-noise ratio, weak anatomical boundaries, and frequent artifacts. We present a unified framework for the LISA 2026 Challenge that performs all three tasks together within one inference pipeline. A network with two coupled streams, built on a 3D U-Net, first reconstructs an enhanced uLF volume and then combines the original and enhanced images for subcortical segmentation. To improve boundary stability, we add an auxiliary class covering brain tissue outside the target structures, derived from whole brain masks. A head conditioned on an artifact graph predicts the seven artifact ratings from reconstruction residuals and frozen segmentation features. We address the scarcity of dense annotations using diffeomorphic registration from atlas to target for label propagation and to regularize anatomical reconstruction. We report validation results across all three tasks.

</details>


### [69] [FAMOS: Feed-Forward 3D Articulation Modeling from Sparse Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20817)
*Kevin Qu,Tao Sun,Massimiliano Viola,Liyuan Zhu,Zhizhuo Zhou,Sayan Deb Sarkar,Konrad Schindler,Iro Armeni*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 FAMOS，一种面向稀疏单目多视角观测的前馈模型，用于从无序的局部点云中预测可动部件分割和关节参数。模型通过多状态关节变换器融合多次观测，并利用观测关节跨度损失强化对跨观测运动信息的利用；同时引入程序化数据生成器扩充训练数据。实验表明，该方法在 PartNet-Mobility、ACD 和 ArtiCraft-10K 上优于多种前馈与优化基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从稀疏单目视角建模铰接物体具有明显困难：单次观测只能揭示部分几何结构和有限的运动证据，而现有前馈方法通常依赖单个观测，并过度依赖类别级形状先验。与此同时，现有数据集在规模和多样性方面有限，制约了模型的泛化能力。因此，需要一种能够联合利用多次观测、适应可变输入数量，并减少对类别先验依赖的方法。

Method: 提出 FAMOS 前馈模型，输入为稀疏、无序且局部的点云集合，输出可动部件分割和关节参数。核心模块是多状态关节变换器（Multi-state Articulation Transformer），通过交替执行状态级注意力与全局注意力，在不同观测之间聚合关节运动线索。模型还引入观测关节跨度目标，对每个部件在输入观测中呈现的运动范围进行监督，促使网络充分利用完整观测集合，并支持从单视图到多视图的可变数量输入。此外，采用程序化数据生成器在训练阶段合成带有自动标注的资产，以提升数据规模与多样性。

Result: 在 PartNet-Mobility、ACD 和 ArtiCraft-10K 三个数据集上的实验显示，FAMOS 相较于前馈方法和基于优化的方法基线均取得稳定改进，说明联合建模多观测、显式监督观测运动跨度以及程序化数据增强能够有效提升铰接物体解析性能。摘要未提供具体指标、提升幅度或消融实验结果。

Conclusion: FAMOS 证明了在稀疏单目观测条件下，联合利用多次部分点云和跨观测运动信息，比仅依赖单观测及类别级形状先验更有效。多状态注意力、观测关节跨度监督和程序化数据生成共同提升了模型对铰接结构的识别与参数估计能力，并使其适用于不同数量的输入观测。

Abstract: Modeling articulated objects from sparse monocular views is challenging because each observation reveals only partial geometry and motion evidence. Most feed-forward methods infer articulation from a single observation and therefore rely heavily on learned category-level shape priors. We present FAMOS, a feed-forward model that predicts movable-part segmentation and joint parameters from a sparse, unordered set of partial point clouds. Our model jointly reasons over multiple observations and naturally supports a variable number of inputs, including a single view. To aggregate articulation cues across observations, we introduce a Multi-state Articulation Transformer with alternating state-wise and global attention. We further propose an observed articulation span objective that supervises the motion range each part exhibits across the input observations, encouraging the model to leverage the full observation set. To overcome the limited scale and diversity of existing datasets, we introduce a procedural data generator that synthesizes self-annotated assets during training. Experiments on PartNet-Mobility, ACD, and ArtiCraft-10K demonstrate consistent improvements over both feed-forward and optimization-based baselines. Project page: https://kevinqu7.github.io/famos

</details>


### [70] [SplashSplat: Reconstructing Splashing Liquids from Real-World Multi-View Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20818)
*Peiyu Liu,Dingxi Zhang,Federico Tombari,Marc Pollefeys,Christina Tsalicoglou,Daniel Barath*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出SplashSplat及首个同步多视角液体飞溅数据集，用于真实液体飞溅的动态三维重建与渲染；方法结合逐帧SDF、水平集输运、拉格朗日载体和局部高斯表示，在真实与合成数据上优于现有动态Gaussian Splatting方法，并支持插帧和风格迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 液体飞溅持续时间极短，液膜会迅速破裂为细丝和液滴，外观受视角影响明显且缺乏稳定纹理，导致传统依赖长期可跟踪特征的三维重建方法难以处理。现有研究主要针对烟雾、合成液体或缓慢形变表面，缺少真实液体飞溅的同步多视角数据集和有效重建方法，因此需要建立真实基准并设计适配瞬态、低纹理流体的表示。

Method: 论文构建包含20个真实场景的数据集，使用7台同步、标定的4K相机以60 fps采集，并提供逐视角液体与容器掩码及固定评测划分。提出SplashSplat：首先由多视角掩码融合得到逐帧液体符号距离场（SDF）；随后通过相邻帧间的水平集输运估计粗粒度速度场；再沿该流场推进拉格朗日载体，根据新观测进行校正，并在覆盖不足处重新采样；最后由载体解码局部Gaussian，用于可微渲染。该设计仅在观测能够约束的位置引入物理结构。

Result: 在真实采集数据和合成基准上，SplashSplat均优于现有先进动态Gaussian Splatting方法，同时生成的运动具有更强的物理合理性，并降低了训练成本。此外，同一表示无需重新优化即可支持时间插值和风格迁移。

Conclusion: 该工作通过数据集与方法的联合贡献，推进了真实液体飞溅的动态三维重建。其核心优势在于以观测驱动的几何和运动约束处理瞬态、低纹理流体，避免对不可可靠估计的物理结构进行过度建模。实验结果表明，SplashSplat在重建质量、运动合理性、训练效率及后续编辑能力方面具有综合优势。

Abstract: A splash lives for a fraction of a second: sheets tear into ligaments and droplets, appearance is view-dependent and nearly textureless, and little persists long enough to track. Reconstruction research has consequently focused on smoke, synthetic liquids, or gently deforming surfaces. To our knowledge, no synchronized multi-view dataset of splashing liquids exists. We therefore introduce a benchmark of 20 real scenes, from coherent streams to violent splashes, captured by seven synchronized, calibrated 4K cameras at 60 fps, with manually refined per-view liquid and container masks and fixed evaluation splits. We further present SplashSplat, built on a single principle: impose physical structure only where the observations can constrain it. Per-frame liquid SDFs fused from the masks provide the geometry, level-set transport between consecutive SDFs yields a coarse velocity field, and Lagrangian carriers advected along this flow, corrected against each new observation and reseeded where coverage is lost, decode local Gaussians for differentiable rendering. SplashSplat outperforms state-of-the-art dynamic Gaussian splatting methods on our real captures and on a synthetic benchmark, with physically more plausible motion and a lower training cost. The same representation supports temporal interpolation and style transfer without re-optimization.

</details>


### [71] [Can 4D Foundation Models Remember?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20819)
*Guangzhao He,Hadar Averbuch-Elor,Wei-Chiu Ma*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 PersistBench，一个利用 360°视频作为全知式真实基准的数据集与评测套件，用于以对象为中心评估 4D 基础模型的视觉记忆能力。评测涵盖物体持续性、运动连续性和外观保持，结果表明现有模型主要具备短期一致性；当物体离开视野后，记忆性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机可控视频模型和 4D 重建模型能够感知并重建动态环境，但尚不清楚它们能否持续记住所感知的信息。已有基准主要依赖像素级指标，并且缺少物体离开视野后的真实参考，因此无法以对象为中心评估视觉记忆。这造成了模型“看见”与“记住”之间的评测空缺。

Method: 作者构建 PersistBench 数据集和指标套件，使用 360°视频提供全视角、全时序的近似全知式真实信息，从而为离开当前视野的物体提供参考。评测从三个方面展开：物体持续性（是否仍能记住物体存在及其身份）、运动连续性（物体运动轨迹和状态是否连贯）、外观保持（物体外观属性是否稳定）。同时在多种类别和不同模型上进行系统评测。

Result: 实验显示，当前模型只能维持较短时间的视觉一致性；一旦物体离开视野，其对象级记忆能力会明显退化，在物体持续性、运动连续性和外观保持方面均暴露出不足。研究还表明，像素层面的局部生成质量并不等价于稳健的视觉记忆。

Conclusion: PersistBench揭示了当前 4D 基础模型与稳健视觉记忆之间的明显差距，说明“看见并不等于记住”。该基准为未来模型改进提供了对象级、可参考真实信息的评测工具和发展方向。

Abstract: Perceiving and remembering the visual world is fundamental to navigating and interacting with our environment. Current 4D foundation models, such as camera-controllable video models or 4D reconstruction models, can perceive and reconstruct dynamic environments, but how well they remember what they have perceived remains an open question. Existing benchmarks largely rely on pixel-level metrics and lack ground truth for objects once they leave the field of view, making them unable to evaluate visual memory in an object-centric manner against references. To fill this gap, we introduce PersistBench, a dataset and metric suite that leverages 360° videos as omniscient ground truth and proposes three evaluation aspects: object permanence, motion continuity, and appearance preservation. Evaluating various models across diverse categories reveals that current models can only maintain short-term consistency that degrades significantly once objects leave the field of view. Our findings highlight the gap between current model capabilities and robust visual memory ("seeing is not remembering"), providing guidance for future development of 4D foundation models. Dataset and code are available on the project page: https://guangzhaohe.com/persistbench.

</details>


### [72] [TouchSight: Bare-Handed Tactile Prediction from Egocentric Video via Generative Visual Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20414)
*Danyan Zhou,Jinxuan Lu,Jiawei Lin,Tianxing Chen,Chuqiao Lyu,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TouchSight利用单目第一视角视频，从手部外观和手-物交互中预测密集的全手接触力；通过压力手套数据与生成式裸手视频缓解训练域和真实场景之间的外观差异，实现无需采集时安装触觉传感器的触觉力估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 触觉信号对于理解物理接触和实现灵巧机器人操作非常关键，但传统触觉采集必须在接触界面部署传感器，成本高、侵入性强且限制数据规模。论文旨在利用易于采集的第一视角视觉数据恢复密集接触力，并解决压力手套训练数据与真实裸手场景之间的外观域差距。

Method: 提出TouchSight单目第一视角视觉框架，使用500小时压力手套记录及大规模手-物交互数据进行训练，以预测全手密集接触力。构建TwinTouch-20H数据集：利用生成式视频模型将带手套记录重新渲染为裸手视频，并更换背景，同时保留原始触觉标签，从而获得20小时成对视觉数据，用于缩小带手套与裸手之间的外观差异。

Result: TouchSight在带手套视频和生成的裸手视频上都能预测密集接触力，并在OakInk2上优于已有接触预测方法。模型能够定性泛化到未见过的数据集中的自然裸手第一视角视频；随着手套监督数据规模增加，性能持续提升。

Conclusion: 研究表明，仅依靠第一视角视觉即可从视频中恢复密集触觉信号，无需在数据采集时安装触觉仪器。大规模压力手套监督结合生成式裸手数据转换，是实现可扩展视觉触觉学习和机器人灵巧操作的有效路径。

Abstract: Tactile signals provide direct contact and force measurements that are essential for understanding physical interactions and enabling dexterous robotic manipulation. However, tactile sensing requires direct measurement at contact interfaces, making large-scale data collection reliant on intrusive, costly, and restrictive instrumentation. We present TouchSight, a monocular egocentric vision framework for dense full-hand contact force prediction that leverages 500 hours of pressure-glove recordings and extensive hand-object interaction (HOI) data. To address the appearance gap between gloved training data and bare-hand real-world scenarios, we construct TwinTouch-20H: 20 hours of paired visual data in which generative video models re-render gloved recordings as bare-hand observations against new backgrounds while preserving the original measured tactile labels. TouchSight predicts dense force from both gloved and generated bare-hand videos, outperforms prior contact prediction methods on OakInk2, qualitatively generalizes to natural bare-hand egocentric videos from unseen datasets, and improves consistently as glove supervision scales. These results demonstrate that dense tactile signals can be recovered from egocentric vision alone, without tactile instrumentation at capture time.

</details>


### [73] [ERCPMP-Gx: Endoscopic Image and Video Dataset for Morphological, Histopathological, and Genomic Characterization of Colorectal Polyposis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20815)
*Zahra Ghaffari,Massih Bahar,Mojgan Forootan,Ali Darvishi,Hamidreza Bolhasani*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了ERCPMP-Gx，一个面向结直肠息肉病人工智能研究的患者级多模态数据集，整合内镜图像/视频、病理信息及临床报告的生殖系遗传发现，用于遗传性息肉病综合征的识别、表征和鉴别分类。数据集包含约160张图像及配套视频，约80%为经临床和/或遗传学确认的遗传性息肉病综合征病例，约20%为非遗传性息肉及息肉样病变，并已公开发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遗传性息肉病综合征可能是结直肠癌的癌前病变，并与多种肠外肿瘤相关。早期识别和准确分类对于及时诊断、个体化管理及家族成员的精准监测十分重要。然而，现有公共内镜数据集多以单个散发性息肉为组织单位，缺少将患者层面的息肉病表型与组织病理学、胚系遗传信息关联起来的数据资源，限制了人工智能模型对遗传性息肉病综合征进行综合识别和分类。

Method: 作者构建并公开了ERCPMP-Gx多模态数据集。数据主要来源于Olympus EVIS X1内镜系统，涵盖白光内镜（WLE）、窄带成像（NBI）、放大NBI（M-NBI）及近焦NBI等模式，并提供160张内镜图像及相应视频片段。病例约80%属于临床和/或遗传学确认的遗传性息肉病综合征，包括家族性腺瘤性息肉病（FAP）、Peutz-Jeghers综合征（PJS）、幼年性息肉病综合征（JPS）和神经节细胞瘤综合征（GNS）；约20%为非遗传性息肉及形态相似的息肉模拟病变，用于支持鉴别分类。每条记录在可获得时关联标准化内镜标注、代表性组织病理学资料和临床报告的胚系遗传学结果，形成患者级标注框架。

Result: 研究产出一个可公开访问、面向AI应用的患者级多模态数据集ERCPMP-Gx。该数据集将内镜影像与视频、内镜标注、组织病理学及胚系遗传信息进行关联，并同时纳入遗传性和非遗传性、具有形态重叠的病变，为遗传性息肉病综合征的识别、表型表征、分类和鉴别诊断提供数据基础。数据集发布于Mendeley，DOI为10.17632/nzyfc544bx.2。

Conclusion: ERCPMP-Gx填补了现有公共内镜数据集中缺乏患者级、跨模态遗传性结直肠息肉病数据的空白，可用于开发和评估结合内镜、病理与基因组信息的人工智能方法。其潜在价值在于促进遗传性息肉病综合征的早期检测、精准分型和风险分层。不过，摘要未报告具体AI模型、性能指标、样本总数、病例构成的详细统计或外部验证结果，因此目前更应将其理解为数据资源论文，而非AI诊断模型的疗效验证研究。

Abstract: Hereditary polyposis syndromes can be precursor lesions to colorectal cancer and are associated with a broad spectrum of extracolonic tumors. Early identification and accurate classification of these syndromes are essential for timely diagnosis, individualized patient management, and targeted surveillance strategies for affected families. However, public endoscopic datasets are largely organized around the individual sporadic polyp, and none links the polyposis phenotype to histopathology and germline findings at the patient level. Here, we present ERCPMP-Gx, an endoscopic, histopathological, and genomic dataset developed to support the application of artificial intelligence (AI) in the recognition, characterization, and classification of colorectal polyposis. Most procedures were performed using the Olympus EVIS X1 system with white-light endoscopy (WLE), narrow-band imaging (NBI), magnifying NBI (M-NBI), and NBI with near focus modes, yielding 160 images and accompanying video clips. Approximately eighty percent of cases represent clinically and/or genetically confirmed hereditary polyposis syndromes (PG), including familial adenomatous polyposis (FAP), Peutz-Jeghers syndrome (PJS), juvenile polyposis syndrome (JPS), and ganglioneuroma syndrome (GNS), while the remaining twenty percent comprise non-hereditary polyps and polyp-mimicking lesions with overlapping morphological features (Non-PG), included to support differential classification. Each released record is linked, where available, to standardized endoscopic annotations, representative histopathology, and clinically reported germline findings, forming an AI-ready, patient-level annotation framework. The dataset is publicly accessible at Mendeley (https://doi.org/10.17632/nzyfc544bx.2). For the latest updates and further information, readers are referred to the DataBioX website: https://databiox.com.

</details>


### [74] [Paint-Anything: Unified Any-Color Control for Image Generation and Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20816)
*Ji Xie,Dewei Zhou,Xinyu Huang,Zhennan Chen,Xun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 论文提出 Paint-Anything，通过统一的 24 位十六进制颜色提示接口，实现图像生成与编辑中的任意目标颜色控制；同时构建 Paint-500K 数据集和 Any Color Benchmark，用于训练与评估对象级颜色保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 专业图像设计需要精确指定对象颜色，但现有方法通常依赖专用颜色表示或复杂的推理流程，且难以在生成和编辑任务中统一支持任意 24 位十六进制颜色。论文旨在利用大语言模型对十六进制颜色语义的已有能力，建立更简单、通用且精确的颜色控制接口。

Method: 提出 Paint-Anything，共享十六进制颜色提示接口，并通过对象级颜色监督进行训练。首先从真实图像出发，结合对象定位、感知颜色标注和编辑对合成，构建 Paint-500K 数据集。考虑到阴影会使真实图像中的颜色标签仅近似于目标颜色，论文进一步加入纯色锚点，使像素值与对应十六进制颜色完全一致；这些锚点仅用于高噪声时间步，而低噪声训练仍主要使用自然图像，以兼顾颜色精确性和真实图像分布。另提出 Any Color Benchmark，包括 ACBench-T2I 和 ACBench-Edit，分别评估文本到图像生成与图像编辑中的对象级十六进制颜色保真度。

Result: 在 FLUX.2-4B 上，相较基础模型，Paint-Anything 使 ACBench-T2I 和 ACBench-Edit 得分分别提升 85.3% 和 28.3%。消融实验支持所提出训练方案的有效性；此外，在对比方法中取得最高的平均 CompColor 得分。

Conclusion: Paint-Anything 证明了共享的十六进制提示接口可以有效提升生成和编辑任务中的任意颜色控制能力。真实图像监督、纯色锚点和分阶段噪声训练的组合能够显著增强对象级颜色保真度，并为统一评测此类能力提供了数据集和基准。

Abstract: Professional design requires any-color control: the ability to specify an object's target color with any 24-bit hex value for image generation and editing. Prior work has explored color generation, editing, and colorization, but often relies on dedicated color representations or specialized inference procedures. Advances in large language models offer a simpler starting point: even compact models can associate hex values with color semantics. We present Paint-Anything, which learns a shared hex-prompt interface for generation and editing through object-level color supervision. We develop a data pipeline that constructs Paint-500K from real images through object grounding, perceptual color labeling, and editing-pair synthesis. Since shadows make real-image labels only approximate colors, we complement this supervision with pure-color anchors whose pixels exactly match their paired hex values. These anchors are used only at high-noise timesteps, leaving low-noise training to natural images. We further introduce Any Color Benchmark (ACBench), comprising ACBench-T2I and ACBench-Edit, to measure object-level hex color fidelity across both tasks. On FLUX.2-4B, Paint-Anything improves ACBench-T2I and ACBench-Edit scores by 85.3% and 28.3%, respectively, relative to the base model, with ablations supporting the training recipe. It also achieves the highest average CompColor score among the compared methods.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [75] [Modality Discrepancy Transformer for Ambivalence and Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19148)
*Shiyu Luo,Yu Wang,Jiawen Huang,Zhaoxiang Xiao,Chenxi Huang,Qi Zhang,Bin Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出模态差异变换器（MDT），通过显式建模文本、语音和面部等模态之间的冲突与不一致，识别临床视频中的矛盾与犹豫（A/H）状态。在BAH数据集上取得0.7408 Macro F1，并在私有测试榜单上取得0.7368，较最强已发表基线提升超过10个百分点，同时训练时间低于20分钟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 矛盾与犹豫状态会在面部、声音和语言等通道中表现出相互不一致的信号。传统多模态融合通常倾向于整合一致信息并抑制模态冲突，因此可能丢失识别A/H所需的关键线索。论文旨在设计一种能够显式保留、表示并利用跨模态不一致性的融合方法。

Method: 论文基于Bekhouche等人的冲突感知多模态融合框架，提出Modality Discrepancy Transformer（MDT）。模型将原有6-token设计扩展为9-token表示，包括3个模态嵌入、3个模态绝对差异特征，以及通过线性投影学习得到的3个Hadamard乘积差异特征。9个token经过Transformer自注意力建模；同时引入FiLM文本条件调制和LoRA参数高效微调。推理阶段还使用文本引导的后融合分支，将仅文本辅助头的输出与完整多模态输出进行融合。

Result: 在第3届ABAW挑战赛的BAH数据集上，MDT在标注测试集上取得0.7408 Macro F1，在私有排行榜上取得0.7368 Macro F1。该方法相比最强已发表基线提升超过10个百分点，并且在单张GPU上训练时间少于20分钟。

Conclusion: 结果表明，显式编码模态间的绝对差异和乘积差异，能够有效捕捉A/H识别中的跨模态冲突信息。MDT结合Transformer、文本条件调制、LoRA和文本引导后融合，在保持较低训练成本的同时显著提升了多模态识别性能，说明冲突感知融合比单纯抑制模态不一致更适合A/H检测。

Abstract: Ambivalence and hesitancy (A/H) are affective states in which individuals express contradictory signals across facial, vocal, and linguistic channels. Automatically recognising A/H in clinical videos requires detecting cross-modal disagreement -- the signal that standard fusion methods suppress. Based on the conflict-aware multimodal fusion framework of Bekhouche et al., we present the Modality Discrepancy Transformer (MDT). MDT enriches the original 6-token design to a 9-token representation comprising three modality embeddings, three absolute-difference features, and three Hadamard-product discrepancy features learned through linear projections. These nine tokens undergo Transformer self-attention, with FiLM-based text-conditioned modulation and LoRA fine-tuning as core architectural components. A text-guided late fusion branch blends a text-only auxiliary head with the full multimodal output at inference. On the BAH dataset from the 3rd ABAW Challenge, MDT achieves 0.7408 Macro F1 on the labelled test split and 0.7368 on the private leaderboard, outperforming the strongest published baseline by over 10 points while training in under 20 minutes on a single GPU.

</details>


### [76] [Subliminal Prompting Beyond Static Geometry: Causal Depth and Multi-Token Confounds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19149)
*Barath Velmurugan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统区分并测量语言模型中“隐性特征传递”相关的四类性质：输出共变、固定输出向量几何相似性、隐藏状态中的可读性，以及隐藏状态对最终答案的因果控制。结果表明，因果控制证据较强，而固定几何相似性和多token测量并不能可靠解释该现象；因此，token entanglement只能受到约束，尚不足以确定训练阶段特征传递的具体机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于subliminal learning和token entanglement的研究，往往将相关但不同的问题混为一谈：输出是否共同变化、固定输出表示是否相似、隐藏状态是否包含可读信息，以及该信息是否真正因果性地控制答案。本文旨在在同一动物—数字提示协议下分别测量这些性质，并检验多token数字评分是否会受到序列长度混淆。

Method: 在Llama-3.1-8B至70B模型中，比较固定输出向量相似性与行为变化的关系；使用固定输出头在不同归一化深度进行读出，评估隐藏状态的观察可读性；在五个深度将一个数字提示的临时答案位置隐藏状态复制到另一个数字提示，观察最终动物评分跟随供体提示还是接收提示，以测试因果控制。进一步检查剩余八层时的控制效果、特异性与身份对照，并在两个Qwen模型中逐token评分所有数字，分析数字宽度和逐token平均造成的长度混淆。

Result: 从Llama-3.1-8B到70B，固定输出向量相似性对行为的预测能力下降，配对均值相关变化为-0.080，95% CI为[-0.127,-0.035]。固定输出头读出在归一化深度AUC上没有明确变化。状态复制实验中，供体控制AUC由0.254升至0.540，配对变化为+0.286，95% CI为[+0.272,+0.300]，且18个概念均有提升。即使保留八个Transformer模块，效果仍存在；特异性和身份对照较小或接近精确。Qwen模型中，对每个数字逐序列评分未能恢复正向的一token关联；逐token平均产生的正向关联在控制数字宽度后消失，说明其主要源于长度混淆。

Conclusion: 不同测量指标反映的是彼此独立的性质，不能互相替代。该研究支持隐藏状态在特定时间位置对最终动物答案具有因果控制作用，但不支持将固定输出几何或未经校正的多token评分直接视为机制证据。总体而言，结果约束了token级解释，却没有识别训练时隐性特征传递的真正机制。

Abstract: Subliminal learning shows that language models can transmit a hidden trait through outputs that appear unrelated to it. One proposed explanation, token entanglement, links animal and number tokens through the model's output vocabulary. Yet existing measurements answer different questions: whether outputs co-vary, fixed output vectors align, an answer can be read from a hidden state, or that state causally controls the answer. We measure each separately in a fixed animal-number prompting protocol. From Llama-3.1-8B to 70B, fixed output-vector similarity predicts behavior less well: the paired mean correlation change is -0.080 (95% CI [-0.127, -0.035]). A fixed output-head readout shows no resolved change in normalized depth AUC. To test control, we copy the temporary answer-position state from one number prompt into another at five depths and measure which prompt the final animal score follows. Donor-control AUC rises from 0.254 to 0.540, a paired change of +0.286 (95% CI [+0.272, +0.300]), with increases for all 18 concepts. The contrast remains with exactly eight transformer blocks remaining, while specificity and identity controls remain small or exact. In two Qwen models, scoring every digit in sequence does not recover the positive one-token association. Per-token averaging instead creates a positive pooled association that disappears after controlling number width, revealing a length confound. Thus, fixed geometry, observational readability, causal timing, and multi-token measurement are distinct properties of this frozen prompting channel. They constrain token-level explanations but do not identify the mechanism of training-time trait transfer.

</details>


### [77] [Sampling Reveals Style: Unsupervised, Training-Free Discovery of Prompt-Conditional Stylistic Axes in LLM Activations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19150)
*Ajit Mallavarapu,Ziwei Gu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的提示条件方法：对同一提示在较高温度下反复采样生成文本，提取并池化模型隐藏激活后进行主成分分析（PCA），再依据主成分两端的代表性生成结果自动命名风格轴。实验表明，该方法能够以较低成本发现与人类感知相关的风格维度，但效果显著依赖模型。Qwen-3.5-4B-Instruct表现最佳，前两个主成分对人类自发提出风格维度的精确率为72.8%、宏平均召回率为43.6%；75.6%的有效性评价认为轴两端生成结果与标签准确匹配，邻近标注者一致率为90.9%。相比之下，DeepSeek-7B-Chat的精确率仅为35.3%，其主成分更多反映结构性而非风格性差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法通常需要带监督的对比数据，才能识别语言模型隐藏表示中与特定提示相关的显著风格维度，成本较高且可扩展性有限。论文旨在构建一种训练-free、提示条件化的探测方法，直接利用模型自身解码产生的变化来发现风格结构，并比较不同模型在风格表示组织上的差异。

Method: 对单个提示在升高的采样温度下反复生成多个补全；提取这些生成过程中的隐藏激活并进行池化；对池化后的激活实施PCA，将主成分视为潜在风格轴；根据每个轴两端对应的极端生成结果自动为风格轴命名。随后通过两阶段人工评估，使用245条人类诱导的风格标注验证轴与人类感知维度的对应关系，并评估极端生成结果是否符合自动生成的标签。同时比较Qwen、Llama和DeepSeek等模型的跨模型表现。

Result: 方法在不同模型上的发现能力差异明显。Qwen-3.5-4B-Instruct效果最强：前两个轴匹配人类自发请求的风格维度时，精确率为72.8%，宏平均召回率为43.6%；75.6%的有效性评分认为轴两端生成内容准确体现标签，邻近标注者一致率达到90.9%。Qwen系列和Llama-3.2-3B能够较好暴露人类显著的风格轴，而DeepSeek-7B-Chat的精确率降至35.3%，其领先主成分主要由结构性差异主导，而非风格差异。

Conclusion: 仅基于模型自身采样方差进行简单PCA，可以作为一种有效、低成本的LLM风格表示探测工具，无需额外训练或监督对比数据。该方法能够发现部分与人类感知一致的风格维度，但其可靠性取决于模型，说明不同LLM对风格结构的编码和组织方式存在显著差异。论文同时表明，主成分并不必然对应风格信息，也可能捕获格式、长度或其他结构性变化，因此跨模型应用时需要进行有效性验证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) encode rich stylistic structure in their hidden activations, but discovering which stylistic dimensions are salient for a given prompt typically requires supervised contrastive data. We present a training-free, prompt-conditional alternative: we repeatedly sample completions of a single prompt at elevated temperature, apply Principal Component Analysis (PCA) to the pooled hidden activations, and label the resulting axes automatically from the pole generations. We validate the discovered axes against 245 human-elicited stylistic annotations in a two-phase study. On our strongest model (Qwen-3.5-4B-Instruct), the top two axes match spontaneously requested human dimensions with 72.8% precision and 43.6% macro-recall, and 75.6% of validity ratings judge the axes' polar generations accurate to their labels, with 90.9% adjacent inter-annotator agreement. Discoverability is strongly model-dependent: both Qwen models and Llama-3.2-3B expose human-salient axes, while DeepSeek-7B-Chat drops to 35.3% precision, its leading components dominated by structural rather than stylistic variance. Simple PCA over a model's own decoding variance is thus an effective, low-cost probe of stylistic structure in LLM representations, one that also exposes sharp cross-model differences in how that structure is organized.

</details>


### [78] [What Users Think of Generative AI: A Cross-Platform NLP Analysis of Trust and Friction in App Store Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19151)
*Md Jafrin Hossain,Umme Nusrat Jahan,Shouvaggo Sharif Shammo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文分析：基于六款生成式人工智能应用的应用商店评论，研究用户感知质量、信任与采用障碍，并结合主题建模、情感分类和统计检验进行跨应用比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究较少以大规模、跨应用数据系统考察生成式人工智能产品的用户感知质量、信任问题和采用障碍，因此需要从真实用户评论中提取可验证的共性与差异。

Method: 收集六款应用在Google Play和Apple App Store上的17,012条英文评论；使用BERTopic进行主题建模、RoBERTa进行情感分类，并采用卡方检验、Kruskal-Wallis检验和多项逻辑回归进行比较分析，同时以300条分层抽样评论进行人工编码验证，并进行Bonferroni校正。

Result: 负面情感主要集中于广告、身份验证、服务器可靠性和订阅价格等主题；不同应用之间的情感分布存在显著差异。Claude同时呈现较高负面情感与较强积极情感，显示出用户评价两极化。研究还提出了用于概括信任与可用性障碍的Trust Friction Score。

Conclusion: 研究为理解消费者生成式人工智能应用中的用户信任、可用性和采用障碍提供了经验证据，并为产品改进和用户体验评估提供了可操作方向。

Abstract: Generative AI (GenAI) applications have achieved rapid consumer adoption, yet little large-scale research examines user-perceived quality, trust, and adoption barriers. We present one of the first cross-application analyses of app store reviews for six major GenAI applications (ChatGPT, Gemini, Microsoft Copilot, Claude, DeepSeek, and Perplexity), comprising 17,012 English-language reviews from Google Play and the Apple App Store. We combine BERTopic topic modeling with RoBERTa sentiment classification and evaluate cross-application differences using chi-square, Kruskal-Wallis, and multinomial logistic regression with Bonferroni correction. Both components are validated against human coding using a stratified sample of 300 reviews. Results show that negative sentiment concentrates in advertising (91%), authentication (89%), server reliability (83%), and subscription pricing (73%). Sentiment differs significantly across applications, with Claude exhibiting the highest negative sentiment (47.7%) alongside a strongly enthusiastic user base, indicating statistically significant polarization. These findings are robust despite unequal review counts across applications. As exploratory observations, a subset of DeepSeek reviews raised geopolitical and data privacy concerns related to its Chinese origin, while a proposed Trust Friction Score summarizes application-specific trust and usability barriers into interpretable dimensions. The study provides validated and actionable evidence on user trust, usability, and adoption barriers in consumer generative AI applications.

</details>


### [79] [FakeSpotter: A content and strategy agnostic Viral Misinformation Detection Tool](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19152)
*Giovanni Spitale,Federico Germani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 FakeSpotter，一种不直接判断内容真假、而是通过识别文本中的结构性误导特征来评估潜在病毒式传播风险的工具。在764篇文本的测试中，短文本和长文本的宏平均F1分别达到0.788和0.793，并提供可解释的特征评分、信号一致性和谨慎指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有误导信息检测工具通常采用真假二分类，或依赖历史样本训练，因此面对新出现的误导叙事时泛化能力和实用性有限。本文旨在建立一种内容与具体误导策略无关的方法，提前识别可能具有高传播风险的文本，同时避免直接裁定其真实性。

Method: FakeSpotter基于理论驱动框架，从语言、叙事、逻辑和批判性思维四个维度提取误导信息的结构性特征。系统通过多次大语言模型评估生成相关信号，并结合面向短文本和长文本的领域特定逻辑回归分类器进行风险估计。其解释层输出特征维度评分、不同信号之间的一致性指标以及谨慎指数，并支持社交媒体舆情监听。

Result: 在包含764篇来自社交媒体和FakeNewsNet的标注文本语料上，FakeSpotter在独立测试集上的宏平均F1为：短文本0.788，长文本0.793。该结果表明，结构性特征能够在不同文本长度下较稳定地识别潜在的误导信息传播风险。

Conclusion: 研究表明，不直接判断真伪、而是识别误导信息结构指纹的方法，可以支持较早、可解释且由人工监督的风险评估。不过，该工具评估的是潜在传播风险而非事实真实性，因此输出应被视为筛查和辅助决策信号，而不能替代事实核查或专家判断。

Abstract: Misinformation detection tools often rely on binary true and false classifications or models trained on historical examples, limiting their usefulness when novel misleading narratives emerge. Here, we present FakeSpotter, a content- and strategy-agnostic tool designed to estimate the viral misinformation risk of textual content by measuring structural fingerprints of misinformation rather than directly adjudicating truthfulness. FakeSpotter operationalizes a theory-driven framework across linguistic, narrative, logical, and critical-thinking dimensions, using repeated LLM assessments and domain-specific logistic regression classifiers for short and long texts. In a labelled corpus of 764 texts from social media and FakeNewsNet, FakeSpotter achieved macro F1 scores of 0.788 for short texts and 0.793 for long texts on a held-out test set. FakeSpotter's interpretive layer provides explainable outputs through feature-based scores, signal agreement, and a caution index, and can be used for social listening. These findings suggest that identifying the structural fingerprints of misinformation can support early, explainable, and human-supervised assessment of potentially viral misinformation.

</details>


### [80] [Stop Removing Stopwords: How an Inherited Preprocessing Default Distorts Legal Text-as-Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19153)
*Gregory M. Dickinson*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过单词级穷举消融，直接评估停用词移除对法律文本分类性能及测量有效性的影响。基于最高法院判例标签与判决文本，研究发现：常见停用词表普遍劣于保留全部词语的基线；即使优化停用词表也没有显著收益；词级元模型无法预测哪些词的移除有帮助。因此，在可解释的法律文本分析中，默认移除停用词缺乏经验依据，保留停用词更符合测量有效性要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法律文本数据研究通常使用TF-IDF与线性分类器，以保持可解释性；但这些流程沿用了信息检索领域的传统预处理默认设置，尤其是停用词移除，而这些设置未必经过面向下游分类准确率的验证。由于预处理会改变模型可见的词汇特征，错误移除可能削弱研究者真正想测量的教义性或意识形态信号，因此有必要直接检验停用词移除是否有效。

Method: 研究将最高法院数据库标签与Caselaw Access Project的判决意见文本匹配，构建两个二分类任务：意识形态方向和宪法性/非宪法性法律类型，样本量分别为7,668篇和7,001篇。研究采用穷举式单词消融：针对约18,500个候选词，逐一移除该词并测量其对下游F1的影响，以近似构造专家能够设计的最优停用词表；同时比较常见通用停用词表、完全不移除词语的基线，并训练词级元模型预测词语移除是否有益。

Result: 常见停用词表在所有测试中都低于不移除词语的基线。即使经过任务导向优化的停用词表，其表现与完全不移除词语相比也没有统计显著差异。基于词级特征训练的元模型无法预测哪些词语的移除会带来收益，说明停用词表的人工筛选缺乏明确可利用的目标。研究覆盖的两个任务基线F1约为0.68和0.92，分别代表较低和较高的F1提升空间。

Conclusion: 对于可解释的法律文本分类，停用词移除不应被视为无害的默认步骤。现有证据支持保留停用词，因为移除它们既没有稳定的预测收益，也可能改变并削弱研究对象本身的文本信号。本文提出的单词级消融框架还可推广到其他继承而来的预处理默认设置，以检验其是否符合具体研究目标和测量有效性要求。

Abstract: Empirical legal scholarship increasingly treats judicial text as data, and much of it still runs on sparse, interpretable pipelines -- TF-IDF features and linear classifiers -- because the textual feature is often the object of study, not merely a means to a prediction. Yet these pipelines inherit a chain of preprocessing defaults from mid-century information retrieval that were never validated against classification accuracy, the most entrenched being stopword removal. This study introduces an exhaustive single-word ablation that measures a preprocessing step's effect directly against the downstream objective, and applies it to stopword removal as the hardest case to dislodge. Matching Supreme Court Database labels to Caselaw Access Project opinion texts, it examines two binary tasks that bracket F1 headroom, ideological direction (no-removal baseline F1 ~ 0.68) and constitutional versus non-constitutional law type (~ 0.92), across 7,668 and 7,001 opinions. For each task the analysis approximates the best stoplist any expert could build, removing each of roughly 18,500 candidate words and measuring the effect directly. Three findings follow: generic stoplists in common use fall below the no-removal baseline in every test; even optimized stoplists are statistically indistinguishable from removing nothing; and meta-models trained on word-level features cannot predict which removals help, so list curation has nothing to target. The method generalizes to any inherited preprocessing default, and the result is a caution specific to interpretable legal text-as-data: a step that silently reshapes which features a model sees can distort the very doctrinal and ideological signal such research exists to recover. Leaving stopwords in place is a question of measurement validity.

</details>


### [81] [Neo-Classic: A Benchmark for Evaluating Linguistic-Aesthetic Reasoning in Classical Chinese Poetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19154)
*Han Zhang,Zihan Gu,Zhiyuan Wang,Tianyi Ma,Jiacheng Lu,Xinyan Zhang,Yuhao Wei,Cheng Hua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Neo-Classic 基准，用当代专家创作且严格合律的古典诗歌与逆向理解任务，检验大语言模型是否真正具备可迁移的语言—审美推理能力。实验表明，模型在当代文本和篇章级层次规划上明显弱于在人类历史语料上的表现，主要只能掌握局部形式模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有古典诗歌基准中的高分可能来自模型对预训练历史语料的记忆、检索或熟悉模式，而不一定代表模型具备可迁移的语言—审美推理能力。因此，研究需要构造避免直接检索、能够检验层级约束满足与全局规划能力的新评测。

Method: 提出 Neo-Classic 评测基准。其核心包括：（1）由当代专家创作、严格遵守格律的分布外（OOS）诗歌数据集，以降低历史语料直接匹配的可能；（2）一组逆向理解探测任务，用于评估模型对诗歌结构和意义的反向推理；（3）五类行为测试，重点考察从局部形式约束到篇章级顺序组织的层级约束满足能力。研究评估了 Qwen3-Max、Gemini-3-Pro、DeepSeek-V3.2 等先进模型，并比较了专家级指导对推理增强模型的影响。

Result: 模型从历史文本迁移到当代文本时，性能下降约20%至50%。在篇章级排序任务中，模型的标准准确率仅为0%至13%，显示其难以处理全局结构。专家级指导能够将推理增强模型的表现提升至36%，但与人类专家仍存在显著差距。总体上，模型能够捕捉局部格律和形式模式，却难以完成全局层级规划。

Conclusion: 当前大语言模型在既有古典诗歌基准上的高准确率，不能充分证明其具备稳健、可迁移的语言—审美推理能力。Neo-Classic 揭示出模型在分布外文本、逆向理解和篇章级组织方面的明显缺陷，说明未来研究应从表层模式匹配转向全局语义规划、层级约束推理及更可靠的审美理解评估。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) achieve high accuracy on established Classical Chinese Poetry benchmarks, it remains challenging to distinguish transferable Linguistic-Aesthetic Reasoning from reliance on familiar pre-training patterns. To address this issue, we introduce Neo-Classic, an evaluation benchmark that combines a constructionist Out-of-Sample (OOS) dataset with a suite of reverse understanding probes. Unlike traditional benchmarks that rely on verification or generation over historical corpora, Neo-Classic comprises strictly metrical poetry authored by contemporary experts, reducing the possibility of direct retrieval. We evaluate state-of-the-art models, including Qwen3-Max, Gemini-3-Pro, and DeepSeek-V3.2, across five behavioral probes designed to test hierarchical constraint satisfaction. Our results reveal two primary limitations. First, a performance gap of 20 to 50 percent emerges when models transition from historical to contemporary texts. Second, models exhibit substantial difficulties in discourse-level ordering tasks, with standard accuracy remaining low (0 to 13 percent). Although expert-level guidance improves the performance of reasoning-enhanced models to 36 percent, a notable gap with human experts persists. These findings suggest that while current LLMs capture local formal patterns, they struggle with global hierarchical planning required for robust Linguistic-Aesthetic Reasoning.

</details>


### [82] [Towards Proactive Detection of User-Side Implicit Conflicts in Human-LLM Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19155)
*Jinqiang Wang,Tao Zhu,Huansheng Ning*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SynUC约束引导的数据合成方法，并构建UC-Bench与UC-Data，用于提升轻量级大语言模型对多轮对话中用户侧隐性冲突的检测能力。实验表明，Qwen3.5-4B使用UC-Data训练后，在UC-Bench上优于更大的通用模型及其他合成数据训练方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮人机对话中，用户后续话语可能与先前意图产生隐性冲突，导致大语言模型误解需求并生成不恰当回复。现有研究主要关注模型侧冲突，对用户侧冲突缺乏系统研究；同时，现有数据合成方法难以建模历史话语与当前话语之间的隐含不兼容关系，因而难以生成可靠标注的隐性冲突样本。本文旨在建立用户侧冲突检测基准，并以有限训练数据提升轻量级模型的检测能力。

Method: 首先构建人工标注的用户侧冲突检测基准UC-Bench。随后提出SynUC，一种约束引导的数据合成方法：将用户侧冲突表示为约束空间中的关系，并借助SPEAKING框架引导可追踪的约束变换，以刻画对话历史到当前话语的冲突演化过程。基于WildChat应用SynUC，构建包含2,487个样本的训练集UC-Data，并用于训练轻量级语言模型。

Result: 初步实验显示，现有大语言模型在用户侧冲突检测任务上表现不足，尤其难以识别依赖对话历史、由隐性不兼容关系引起的冲突。在UC-Bench上，使用UC-Data训练的Qwen3.5-4B优于更大的通用模型Claude Opus 4.8，也优于使用现有数据合成方法生成的数据训练的同架构模型。

Conclusion: 用户侧冲突是多轮对话安全、准确理解用户需求的重要问题，但现有模型仍存在明显识别困难。通过在约束空间中显式建模冲突并进行可追踪的数据合成，SynUC能够生成更有效的训练数据，显著提升轻量级模型的检测性能，验证了约束引导合成在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: In Human-LLM dialogue, follow-up user utterances may implicitly conflict with earlier intents, leading the LLM to misinterpret user needs and generate inappropriate responses. A reliable dialogue system should proactively detect user-side conflicts before generating a response and seek clarification when necessary. However, prior work has largely focused on LLM-side conflicts, leaving user-side conflicts underexplored. To fill this gap, we construct UC-Bench, a human-annotated benchmark for evaluating user-side conflict detection. Preliminary experiments show that existing LLMs struggle with this task, especially when conflicts arise from implicit incompatibilities grounded in dialogue history. To improve lightweight LLMs with limited training data, we investigate data synthesis for user-side conflict detection. Existing synthesis methods do not explicitly model the implicit incompatibilities between historical and current user utterances, making it difficult to capture the evolution of conflicts and to generate reliably labeled implicit conflict samples. We propose SynUC, a constraint-guided synthesis method that represents user-side conflicts in a constraint space and uses the SPEAKING framework to guide traceable constraint transformations. Applying SynUC to WildChat, we construct UC-Data, a user-side conflict training set containing 2,487 samples. On UC-Bench, Qwen3.5-4B trained on UC-Data outperforms larger general-purpose LLMs such as Claude Opus 4.8, as well as the same backbone trained on data synthesized by existing methods.

</details>


### [83] [Reflective Recovery: A Self-Supervised Method for Reasoning by Learning from Mistakes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19156)
*Qirui Chen,Renjie Pi,Jiahui Gao,Lingpeng Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Reflective Recovery，一种利用失败推理轨迹进行自监督恢复训练的方法，使大语言模型学习识别并纠正中间步骤错误，从而缓解正例扩展导致的 Scaling Collapse，并提升复杂数学推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主流模仿学习主要依赖完整且正确的推理轨迹。当问题集合有限时，继续增加正向示例无法带来持续性能提升，出现 Scaling Collapse。此外，模型在推理过程中难以保证每一步都正确，一旦产生错误，往往无法恢复，并会受到累积错误的进一步误导。因此，需要一种不依赖外部批评器或奖励模型、能够直接训练模型从错误状态中恢复的方法。

Method: 提出 Reflective Recovery 自监督训练框架。该方法从模型失败的推理轨迹中提取初始片段，将这些可能包含错误的片段与问题提示拼接，再引导模型生成有效解答，由此构造恢复训练数据。训练目标从单纯模仿正确结果转向学习识别错误、修正错误并继续推理。

Result: 在多个基准测试上取得显著提升。以 DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B 为例，AIME 2025 准确率从 30.0% 提升至 37.5%，Minerva 准确率从 37.6% 提升至 47.8%。进一步分析表明，该方法能够突破 Scaling Collapse，并促使模型产生涌现式自我纠错行为。

Conclusion: Reflective Recovery 证明了失败推理轨迹不仅是错误样本，也可以转化为训练模型恢复能力的有效数据。该方法在无需外部批评器或奖励模型的情况下增强了模型的过程性推理、自我纠错和错误恢复能力，推动训练范式由面向结果的记忆转向面向过程的反思推理。

Abstract: Data-driven fine-tuning is widely adopted to enhance reasoning in Large Language Models (LLMs) due to its simplicity and efficiency. However, mainstream imitation learning methods that rely exclusively on perfect reasoning trajectories suffer from a Scaling Collapse: when the problem set is limited, increasing positive examples fails to yield continuous improvement. However, during inference, an LLM can not guarantee that every intermediate step is correct and is therefore prone to errors. Once such errors arise, the LLM often struggles to recover and may be further misled by the accumulation of previous mistakes. To address this, we propose Reflective Recovery, a simple yet effective self-supervised approach that transforms failed reasoning attempts into recovery training data. Specifically, we extract initial segments of failed trajectories, concatenate them with prompts, and use them to guide the LLM toward valid solutions. Because these segments from failed trajectories are likely to contain errors, this process teaches models to recognize and correct mistakes during reasoning, enabling recovery from erroneous states without relying on external critics or reward models. Evaluated on extensive benchmarks, Reflective Recovery significantly improves performance. On DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B, it boosts accuracy from 30.0% to 37.5% on AIME 2025 and from 37.6% to 47.8% on Minerva. More importantly, analyses demonstrate that it breaks the scaling collapse barrier and enables models to develop emergent self-correction behaviors, representing a paradigm shift from outcome-oriented memorization to process-oriented reflective reasoning.

</details>


### [84] [VisKG-LM: Compiling Knowledge Graphs into Visual Memory for Multiple-Choice Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19158)
*Yixin Peng,Er Jin,Shiwei Luo,Diego Collarana,Stefan Decker*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: VisKG-LM将传统在线图神经网络编码改为离线缓存的视觉知识记忆：把候选子图序列化为带关系标签的路径并渲染成保留分支结构的图像，离线编码后供语言模型最终层读取。在CommonsenseQA、OpenBookQA和MedQA-USMLE上分别较GreaseLM提升1.2、0.8和4.3个百分点；较相同路径输入的文本基线提升4.2、6.5和5.1个百分点，并以约4亿在线参数达到或超过7B参数GraphVis。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱问答方法通常在在线推理路径中使用图神经网络反复编码检索子图。同一知识图谱不变，但子图会在训练轮次、随机种子和评测过程中被重复编码，造成计算浪费。论文旨在验证能否将检索到的知识子图一次性离线编译为只读记忆，同时保留图结构信息，并降低在线推理成本。

Method: VisKG-LM将每个候选对应的检索子图序列化为Relation-Labeled Paths，再将这些路径渲染为二维图像，使布局保留路径的分支结构。图像特征只需离线编码并缓存。在线推理时，语言模型仅根据文本对问题和候选进行上下文建模，最后一层再读取缓存的视觉记忆，同时利用全局布局和局部关系细节。该设计将图信息注入延后到文本理解完成之后，并与仅使用相同路径文本化结果的文本对照模型比较。

Result: 在CommonsenseQA、OpenBookQA和MedQA-USMLE测试集上，VisKG-LM相比GreaseLM分别提升1.2、0.8和4.3个百分点；与GraphVis这一7B参数视觉语言模型相比，VisKG-LM以约400M在线参数实现持平或更优表现。相对于接收完全相同Relation-Labeled Paths的文本基线，VisKG-LM分别提升4.2、6.5和5.1个百分点，说明视觉记忆接口带来的收益不只是路径文本化本身。

Conclusion: 实验表明，检索子图可以被预编译为可复用的视觉记忆，并在推理阶段作为只读外部记忆使用。该方案在保持或提升准确率的同时减少在线图传播与重复编码开销，支持将编译式视觉记忆作为在线知识图谱传播的替代方案。不过，摘要未说明离线存储成本、缓存更新策略、图像布局对性能的敏感性，以及在知识图谱变化或未见结构上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs are usually integrated into question answering by encoding a retrieved subgraph with a graph neural network and fusing it with the language model in the online inference path. The same subgraph is therefore re-encoded from scratch every time a pair is scored, across training epochs, seeds, and evaluation runs, even though the knowledge graph never changes. We ask whether the retrieved knowledge graphs can instead be compiled once, offline, and then accessed as read-only memory. VisKG-LM shows that it can, by decoupling graph encoding from language reasoning. It serializes each retrieved candidate-specific subgraph as Relation-Labeled Paths and renders the result as an image whose two-dimensional layout preserves the branching structure of the paths. Each image is encoded once, offline, and cached for reuse. At inference, the language model contextualizes the question and candidate from text alone, and only its final layer consults the cached visual memory, reading both its global layout and its local relational detail. The graph information thus enters only after the text has been understood. On the test sets of CommonsenseQA, OpenBookQA, and MedQA-USMLE, VisKG-LMimproves over GreaseLM by $1.2$, $0.8$, and $4.3$ points, respectively, while matching or surpassing GraphVis, a $7$B vision-language model, with only about $400$M online parameters. Against a matched text-only control that receives the identical Relation-Labeled Paths, it gains $4.2$, $6.5$, and $5.1$ points across the three benchmarks. These gains show that the complete visual-memory interface adds value beyond path textualization alone and support compiled visual memory as an alternative to online graph propagation.

</details>


### [85] [Advantage Scale Calibration Imbalance in Group-Relative Optimization under Low-Variance Rewards: Diagnosis and Bounded Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19164)
*Fei Ding*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究 verifier-style RLVR 中组内相对优势尺度的校准问题，提出三向优势尺度校准接口、Reward-Resolution Protocol 与 MaxNorm-AC，以过滤不可分辨的小扰动并稳定恢复可信的小幅奖励差异；实验表明 MaxNorm-AC 在多种模型架构和数学、代码推理任务上优于强健尺度基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组相对优化方法常将优势尺度视为实现细节，但不同归一化分母会同时影响奖励分支强度、提示级批次权重以及等效 KL 校准。低方差情形尤其存在两类不同问题：不可分辨的微小抖动不应被当作偏好信号，而可信但幅度较小的奖励差异又不应被忽略或因尺度放大而失真。因此，论文旨在建立统一的尺度分析框架，并在保持 KL 稳定性的同时学习有效的小幅差异。

Method: 论文提出 advantage-scale three-way calibration interface，指出同一组内尺度分母会共同决定奖励分支强度、prompt-level batch weight，以及将奖励分支还原到原始基数尺度后产生的有效 KL 校准。基于该接口，提出 Reward-Resolution Protocol，用于过滤低于分辨率的奖励差异；提出 MaxNorm-AC，在可信的非零差异上进行有界的基数恢复，避免标准差归一化对微小差异产生无界放大，并截断低方差下的逆尺度尾部。论文还比较 RLOO、Dr.GRPO 与 GRPO 在这些情形下的行为。

Result: 理论分析表明，RLOO 和 Dr.GRPO 可能使可信的小奖励差异被 KL 项主导；GRPO 的标准差分母则可能无界放大小差异。实验覆盖 dense 与 MoE 架构，以及数学和代码推理任务。结果显示，MaxNorm-AC 相比最强的 robust-scale 基线取得更好性能，同时有效截断低方差场景下的逆尺度极端值。摘要未提供具体数值、显著性检验或各任务的单独提升幅度。

Conclusion: 优势尺度不应仅被视为实现细节，而应作为奖励强度、批次权重和 KL 校准的联合控制接口。区分不可分辨抖动与可信小幅差异，并分别进行过滤和有界恢复，可以在避免噪声偏好与尺度爆炸的同时保留有效学习信号。MaxNorm-AC 是这一原则下的实用方案，且在多架构、多推理任务上表现出优于稳健尺度基线的效果。

Abstract: In verifier-style RLVR, group-relative optimization often treats advantage scale as an implementation detail. This paper separates two low-variance cases: sub-resolution jitter that should not become a preference signal, and credible but small cardinal gaps that should be learned without distorting KL calibration. We propose an advantage-scale three-way calibration interface: the same within-group scale denominator simultaneously determines the reward-branch strength, prompt-level batch weight, and the effective KL calibration induced when the reward branch is re-expressed on the original cardinal scale. This interface explains why RLOO / Dr.GRPO can let credible small gaps become KL dominated, whereas GRPO's standard-deviation denominator can amplify tiny gaps without bound. Based on this interface, we further introduce the Reward-Resolution Protocol and MaxNorm-AC, respectively filtering sub-resolution gaps and providing bounded cardinal recovery on credible nonzero gaps. Across dense / MoE architectures and math / code reasoning, MaxNorm-AC improves over the strongest robust-scale baseline while truncating the low-variance inverse-scale tail.

</details>


### [86] [To Memories and Beyond: From Remembering to Knowing You across Long-Term Multimodal Personal Archives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19167)
*Wenqi Zhou,Zhuorui Yu,Kaiao Wen,Hao Zheng,Xinyi Zheng,Peiran Wu,Enmin Zhou,Chi-Hao Wu,Junxiao Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 ReaLMem 真实世界长期多模态记忆基准与 ChronoProfiler 时间加权画像模块，用于评估和提升 AI 对用户多年视觉历史、偏好演化及个性化决策的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有长期记忆基准主要基于合成、文本数据，缺少真实的多年视觉档案、第一人称主观标注及事件之间的因果和时间联系，因此只能评估浅层事实回忆，无法充分检验个性化 AI 对用户长期经历和偏好变化的建模能力。

Method: 构建 ReaLMem：基于真实多年个人视觉档案，并配套第一人称主观标注；按照认知难度设置三层任务：事实回忆、人格/用户画像推断和预测性个性化。提出 ChronoProfiler，通过计算用户属性的时间稳定性得分，将其作为显著性先验，处理随时间变化的偏好冲突，并组合多个同时有效的偏好以支持复杂个性化决策。

Result: 在 ReaLMem 上对前沿多模态大语言模型和记忆系统进行广泛评测。结果显示，预测性个性化始终是性能瓶颈；多模态大模型与记忆系统之间存在明显的能力差距和不同瓶颈；高质量且融入时间信息的用户表示能够显著提升个性化效果。

Conclusion: ReaLMem 提供了更真实、 multimodal 且纵向的长期记忆评测环境，ChronoProfiler 则以较简单的时间建模机制有效增强个性化。二者为面向终身陪伴型 AI 的长期记忆与个性化研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: As AI systems evolve into personalized digital companions, a central capability is reasoning over a user's long-term personal history: not merely storing past events, but tracking longitudinal experiences and evolving preferences. Progress here is bottlenecked by evaluation, existing long-term memory benchmarks are largely synthetic and text-only, they overlook the visual records that anchor everyday human memory, lack the authentic and causally connected longitudinal data that real personalization demands, and consequently remain confined to shallow factual recall. We introduce ReaLMem (Real-world Long-term Multimodal Memory), the first benchmark built from authentic multi-year personal visual archives, paired with first-person subjective annotations. ReaLMem evaluates models across three cognitive tiers of increasing difficulty: factual recall, persona inference, and predictive personalization. We further propose ChronoProfiler, a temporal-weighting profiling module that computes temporal stability scores for user attributes and applies them as a salience prior, resolving conflicts among temporally inconsistent preferences and helping models compound multiple co-active preferences in complex personalized decisions. Extensive evaluation of frontier multimodal large language models (MLLMs) and memory systems on ReaLMem reveals predictive personalization as a consistent ceiling, exposes clear performance gaps and bottlenecks between MLLMs and memory systems, and shows that high-quality, temporally informed representations substantially improve personalization. Together, ReaLMem and ChronoProfiler provide an authentic testbed and a simple, effective mechanism for long-term personalization, laying a foundation for future research on lifelong AI companions.

</details>


### [87] [YNU-HPCC at SemEval-2025 Task 11: Bridging the Gap in Text-Based Emotion Using Multiple Prediction Headers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19238)
*Hao Yang,Jin Wang,Xuejie Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍YNU-HPCC团队参加SemEval-2025 Task 11子任务A的系统。系统基于RoBERTa，并采用单情绪预测头及全量英文翻译数据进行训练，官方综合得分为0.44。实验表明，单头逐情绪预测和统一翻译为英文的数据处理策略优于多头联合预测及原始多语言数据训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Task 11旨在弥合文本与情绪之间的差距，涉及多语言文本情绪识别。由于不同语言、数据分布以及多情绪联合建模可能影响模型性能，作者希望探索更有效的模型输出结构和数据预处理方式，以提升跨语言情绪分类效果。

Method: 作者使用RoBERTa作为基础编码器，并改造输出层，使模型一次处理一个情绪，而不是使用六个预测头同时预测六种情绪。为降低多语言处理复杂度，作者将整个数据集通过Google Translate统一翻译成英文，再进行模型训练和推理。同时结合概率分析与注意力分析，比较单预测头与六头联合预测，以及英文翻译数据与原始数据的效果。

Result: 系统在包含所有语言的官方评测中取得0.44分。分析结果显示：（1）单一预测头逐个预测情绪的表现优于六个预测头同时预测六种情绪；（2）在统一翻译成英文的数据上训练优于直接使用原始多语言数据。代码已公开在GitHub。

Conclusion: 研究表明，在该任务中，简化情绪预测结构并统一输入语言能够有效提升性能。结果支持将多语言情绪识别转化为统一英文情绪分类的方案，同时说明输出头设计对多标签或多情绪任务具有重要影响。但该方法依赖机器翻译，可能引入翻译误差，且摘要未说明不同语言、情绪类别及翻译质量对结果的具体影响。

Abstract: This paper describes the participation of the YNU-HPCC team in subtask A of task 11, Bridging the Gap in Text-Based Emotion at SemEval-2025. Our best-performing system employs the RoBERTa (Robustly Optimized BERT Approach) model, an improved version of BERT that utilizes the Transformer encoder architecture. We enhanced the output head to allow the model to process one emotion simultaneously. We obtained the official ranking score (0.44), including results from all languages. The entire dataset was translated into English using Google Translate to facilitate subsequent processing. Through probabilistic and attention analyses, we found that (I) a single prediction head performs better than six heads predicting six emotions simultaneously, and (II) training on a uniformly translated English dataset yields better results than using the original dataset. The code is available at: https://github.com/BGWH123/Semeval-2025-task11.

</details>


### [88] [Why Pretraining Fails to Share Cross-Lingual Knowledge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19291)
*Adam Gaber,Uriel Dolev,Elisabeth Fittschen,Bobby Cheng,Yuval Marton,Leshem Choshen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多语言大语言模型跨语言知识迁移能力不足的根本原因。通过大规模预训练与受控双语实验，作者发现：彼此不重叠的词元空间会导致知识分区，即使是同一种语言的两个副本也无法充分共享知识。将不同语言映射到共享词元空间后，跨语言知识泛化显著提升，最高可恢复本语言学习效率的12.6%，达到基线的14倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究已观察到大语言模型的跨语言知识迁移能力明显弱于人类多语者，但这种局限在多语言预训练过程中如何产生，仍缺乏明确解释。本文旨在识别导致知识跨语言泛化受阻的关键因素，并提出一种简单有效的改进方法。

Method: 作者分别预训练3.6亿参数和70亿参数的大语言模型，考察跨语言知识泛化能力及其在标准干预下的表现。随后构建受控双语预训练环境：使用同一种语言的两个完全相同副本，共享相同文本和词元切分方式，但将它们映射到彼此不相交的词元空间，以隔离词元空间差异的影响。在此基础上，作者进一步采用逐词翻译，将不同语言映射到共享词元空间，并评估其对知识迁移效率的影响。

Result: 实验表明，跨语言知识泛化能力的不足在预训练阶段就已形成，并且在标准干预后仍然存在。受控实验显示，仅仅使用不相交的词元空间，就足以在相同语言的两个副本之间造成知识分区，说明词元空间不重叠是跨语言知识泛化的基础性障碍。将语言映射到共享词元空间后，跨语言知识泛化得到显著改善，最高恢复本语言学习效率的12.6%，约为基线方法的14倍。

Conclusion: 本文将不相交词元空间确定为多语言模型跨语言知识迁移受限的重要根因之一。研究说明，改进语言间词元共享关系，而不仅是增加数据或调整训练过程，可能是提升跨语言知识泛化能力的有效方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have made remarkable progress in the processing and modeling of many languages. Yet, unlike human multilinguals, they exhibit surprisingly limited cross-lingual knowledge transfer. While this limitation is well documented, its origins during multilingual training remain unclear. We pretrain 360M- and 7B-parameter LLMs and show that poor cross-lingual knowledge generalization emerges during pretraining and persists under standard interventions. To isolate its cause, we employ a controlled bilingual pretraining setting using two copies of the same language, sharing identical text and token segmentation, but mapped to disjoint token spaces. We find that disjoint tokens alone are enough to induce knowledge compartmentalization, even between identical copies of the same language, establishing disjoint token spaces as a fundamental barrier to cross-lingual knowledge generalization. Guided by this understanding, we suggest mapping languages into a shared token space by simple word-wise translation and find it substantially improves cross-lingual knowledge generalization, recovering up to 12.6\% of native-language learning efficiency --- 14$\times$ the baseline.

</details>


### [89] [A frontend-backend architecture for tool calls in full-duplex speech models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19334)
*Ke Hu,Slyne Deng,Chen Chen,Elena Rastorgueva,Edresson Casanova,Punit Kumar,Dharmendra Choudhary,Nikhil Srihari,Ameya Sunil Mahabaleshwarkar,Viet Anh Trinh,Slim Essid,Oluwatobi Olabiyi,Zhehuai Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种前端—后端协同架构，使全双工语音到语音模型能够借助文本后端大语言模型完成工具调用，同时保持自然的低延迟对话能力。系统在工具调用召回率、无关调用拒绝和综合基准测试上表现良好，说明后端委派具有模块化和有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有全双工语音到语音模型擅长自然、低延迟的实时交互，但工具调用和复杂语音代理任务能力不足。直接大幅修改前端语音模型可能破坏轮次管理、打断处理和实时性，因此需要一种改动较小、可复用文本大模型能力的工具调用方案。

Method: 采用前端—后端架构：全双工语音到文本前端学习输出委派标记，并将流式自动语音识别转录结果发送给文本后端大语言模型，由后端执行工具调用。后端工具结果通过轻量级的“预填充—重复”机制注入前端，再由流式文本到语音模块合成并输出给用户。该设计尽量保留前端原有的全双工轮次交互、打断处理和低延迟特性。

Result: 在单轮工具调用评测中，系统取得92%—97%的工具调用召回率，工具调用预测性能具有竞争力，并以81.2%的准确率拒绝无关调用。使用更大的后端模型（如Qwen3-235B-A22B）后，在Full-Duplex-Bench-V3上相较开源和闭源模型达到有竞争力的结果；在EVA-Bench上显著优于GPT-realtime-mini和Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct。

Conclusion: 后端委派是一种有效且模块化的方法，可将自然的全双工语音交互与较强的智能体工具调用能力结合起来。其核心优势是在较少修改前端模型的前提下，利用强大的文本后端提升任务执行能力，同时维持实时语音交互体验。

Abstract: Full-duplex speech-to-speech (S2S) models provide natural, low-latency conversational interaction and would benefit from the ability to use external tools and complete voice-agent tasks. We propose a frontend-backend architecture where a duplex speech-to-text frontend learns to emit a delegation token and forwards streaming ASR transcripts to a text-based backend LLM for tool calls. Tool-call results from the backend are injected back into the frontend through a lightweight prefill-and-repeat mechanism and then synthesized using streaming TTS to the user. Our approach largely preserves regular duplex turn-taking, interruption handling, and low-latency interaction as it requires minimal modifications to the frontend model. In a single-turn tool-call evaluation, our system achieves 92-97% tool-call recall, competitive tool-call prediction performance, and 81.2% accuracy in rejecting irrelevant calls. When equipped with a larger backend (e.g., Qwen3-235B-A22B), our system achieves competitive results on Full-Duplex-Bench-V3 compared to open and closed source models, and significantly outperforms GPT-realtime-mini and Qwen3-Omni-30B-A3B-Instruct on EVA-Bench. These results demonstrate that backend delegation is an effective and modular approach for combining natural duplex speech interaction with strong agentic tool-call capabilities.

</details>


### [90] [The Role of Fine-grained Harm Signals in LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19366)
*Soyeon Park,Seogyeong Jeong,Sunwoo Kim,Alice Oh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究大语言模型中风险类别特异性有害表征在共享一般有害性之外的作用。作者通过去除共享一般有害性成分，构造与一般有害性正交的类别残差，并在3个指令微调模型、11类风险上进行激活引导。结果显示，类别残差对有害性编码和拒答的影响具有类别差异；前者跨模型较稳定，后者更依赖模型。类别残差还能在后续层增强模型与一般有害性表征的内部对齐，说明正交方向仍可能通过层间传播放大相关概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究发现，LLM的内部有害性表征同时包含跨风险类别共享的一般有害性成分和类别特异性成分，但类别特异性成分在安全机制中的独立作用尚不清楚。本文旨在回答：在排除共享一般有害性后，类别残差是否仍编码有害性、是否诱发拒答，以及是否会影响模型后续的安全相关内部表征。

Method: 作者在每一层从各风险类别的有害性表征中移除共享的一般有害性表征，得到与一般有害性正交的类别残差。随后对3个指令微调LLM的11类风险进行激活引导，评估类别残差对有害性编码、拒答行为以及后续层内部一般有害性对齐程度的影响，并比较不同类别和模型之间的模式。

Result: 类别残差是否编码有害性因风险类别而异，且这种类别间模式在不同模型之间较为一致。类别残差是否诱发拒答同样存在类别差异，但其模式更依赖具体模型。进一步地，类别残差能够提高模型在后续层与共享一般有害性表征的内部对齐。

Conclusion: LLM安全性不能仅由共享的一般有害性表征解释，还需要纳入更细粒度的风险类别残差。研究还表明，某一层中与某概念正交的方向，仍可能通过后续层的表征变换促进该概念的放大，这对理解层间表征传播和激活引导机制具有一般意义。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that internal harmfulness representations in large language models vary across risk categories, while sharing a common general harm representation component. This raises a question about the role of the category-specific component beyond general harm representation in LLM safety. To answer this question, we isolate the category-specific component by removing shared general harmfulness representation from each categorical harmfulness representation, yielding a category residual that is orthogonal to general harmfulness at every layer. Using activation steering with category residuals across 11 risk categories in 3 instruction-tuned LLMs, we find that whether category residuals encode harmfulness varies across categories, and that this category-wise pattern is similar across models. Whether category residuals induce refusal also varies across categories, but this category-wise pattern is more model-dependent. We also find that category residuals increase LLMs' downstream internal alignment with shared general harmfulness representation. Together, these findings demonstrate that more fine-grained category residuals should also be considered beyond shared general harmfulness representation to fully understand LLM safety. More broadly, our findings show that even a direction orthogonal to a concept at one layer can contribute to the concept's downstream amplification.

</details>


### [91] [Chain-of-Thought Entropy as a Reliability Signal: A Preregistered Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19606)
*Theodore O. Cochran*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究对“链式思考熵轨迹形状比总熵下降幅度更能预测答案正确性”的结论进行大规模、预注册独立复现。结果支持形状信号，但表明幅度信号依赖模型与基准；最终步熵在多数比较中优于二元形状指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原研究关于形状信号的证据较完整，但关于总熵下降幅度的结论主要来自单一模型、单一随机种子和300道题的实验，存在可重复性与外推性不足。因此，本研究旨在检验两类熵指标能否在更大规模、更多模型和不同基准上稳定复现，并识别协议与设置依赖。

Method: 研究在任何验证性运行前于OSF预注册，使用完整GSM8K和MATH-500测试集，测试4个开放权重模型，其中包括原研究未覆盖的推理蒸馏模型。研究比较链式思考熵轨迹的形状指标（单调/非单调及分级违规计数）与总熵下降幅度，并报告正确率差异、秩相关、ROC面积和风险-覆盖面积；同时考察7项协议差异及4类原研究未报告的协议依赖。

Result: 形状信号成功复现。在锚定模型上，单调与非单调链的准确率差异在GSM8K为+9.6个百分点、MATH-500为+27.5个百分点。总熵下降与正确性的秩相关在GSM8K为-0.018、MATH-500为+0.414，显示其表现随基准而变。在推理蒸馏模型上，二元形状信号仅约对百分之一的链触发，样本过少，无法估计预注册对比；但分级违规计数仍具预测性。探索性分析显示，最终步熵在全部8个模型-基准组合中的ROC面积均优于二元形状标志，在风险-覆盖面积上有6或7个组合优于后者，具体取决于原研究未明确的积分区间。

Conclusion: 研究支持链式思考熵轨迹形状具有跨模型、跨基准的预测价值，但不支持将总熵下降幅度视为稳定、普适的信号；后者明显依赖实验设置。结果还表明，简单的最终步熵可能比二元形状指标更有效，因此未来工作应明确评估区间、改进形状指标，并系统报告模型、基准和协议依赖。

Abstract: This empirical study is an independent reproduction of the dissociation Zhao reported in 2026. The shape of a large language model's chain-of-thought entropy trajectory predicts whether the final answer is correct, while the magnitude of its total entropy drop does not. The dissociation merits reproduction because the magnitude half rests on a single 300-problem run with one model at one seed, while the shape half was reported at full scale on both benchmarks and on a second model family. Registered at OSF before any confirmatory run, the reproduction crosses the complete GSM8K and MATH-500 benchmark test sets with four open-weight models including one reasoning-distilled model of a kind the original did not test. The shape signal replicates. The magnitude signal divides by setting. On the anchor model the accuracy gap between monotone and non-monotone chains is +9.6 percentage points on GSM8K and +27.5 on MATH-500, while the rank correlation of the total entropy drop with correctness is -0.018 on GSM8K and +0.414 on MATH-500. On the reasoning-distilled model the binary form of the shape signal fires on about one chain in a hundred, too few to estimate the registered contrast, while the graded violation count remains predictive there. In an exploratory comparison the final-step entropy alone outperforms the binary shape flag in all eight model-by-benchmark cells by ROC area, and in six or seven by the risk-coverage area the original reports, depending on an integration range the original does not state. The study contributes a reproduction of the shape signal at full test-set scale under seven documented protocol differences, a map of the settings where the magnitude signal holds and fails, and measurements of four protocol dependencies the original does not report.

</details>


### [92] [Semantic Layer Induction from Raw Telemetry via Hierarchical LLM and RAG Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19615)
*Yuanzhe Jia,Ali Anaissi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端框架，从应用原始日志中自动构建业务语义层。方法通过两阶段语义抽象，结合大语言模型、行业知识、数据精炼、混合检索、多阶段过滤、语义聚类和规范化命名，在无需标注数据或人工规则的情况下，将噪声复杂的遥测事件转化为可维护、可评估的业务节点与特征。生产级评估显示，语义质量由50分提升至80分以上，维护工作量降低80%，噪声过滤率达74%，LLM-as-Judge评估的Cohen’s kappa为0.87。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代应用产生海量、异构且含噪的原始遥测数据，但将这些事件流准确映射为业务指标和可执行洞察仍十分困难。现有流程依赖数据工程师和分析师手工处理语义不一致、解析逻辑及脆弱的数据到业务KPI映射，导致维护成本高、扩展性差且质量难以持续保证。因此，本文旨在解决从原始遥测数据自动归纳业务语义层这一问题。

Method: 框架采用两阶段语义抽象。第一阶段利用大语言模型推断高层业务特征，并结合特定行业知识增强语义识别。第二阶段通过结构化流水线生成细粒度业务节点，具体包括数据精炼、混合检索、多阶段过滤、语义聚类和规范化命名。此外，系统集成LLM-as-Judge机制进行持续质量评估。整体方法不依赖标注训练数据，也不需要人工编写规则。

Result: 在生产规模遥测数据上的评估表明：人工评估的语义质量从50分提升至80分以上；维护工作量降低80%；74%的噪声被过滤；集成式LLM-as-Judge评估达到0.87的Cohen’s kappa，说明评估结果具有较高一致性。

Conclusion: 本文验证了从应用原始日志自动构建业务语义层的可行性，能够显著提升语义质量、降低维护成本并增强噪声处理与持续质量保障能力。其主要创新在于提出并解决了原始遥测到业务语义层归纳这一新问题，并实现了无需标注数据和人工规则工程的可扩展方案。

Abstract: Modern applications generate massive volumes of raw telemetry data, but translating those noisy, heterogeneous event streams into actionable business insights remains a fundamental challenge. Data engineers and analysts expend substantial effort reconciling semantic discrepancies, hand-crafting parsing logics, and maintaining fragile mappings between raw data and business KPIs. In this paper, we present an end-to-end framework that fully automates the construction of a business semantic layer from application raw logs. Our approach introduces a two-stage semantic abstraction: first, high-level business features are identified via LLM inference augmented with domain-specific industry knowledge; second, fine-grained business nodes are derived through a structured pipeline comprising data refinement, hybrid retrieval, multi-stage filtering, semantic clustering, and canonical naming. Evaluation on production-scale telemetry demonstrates that our system improves human-assessed semantic quality from 50 to 80+ on a 100-point scale, reduces maintenance effort by 80%, filters out 74% of noise, and achieves 0.87 Cohen's kappa via an integrated LLM-as-Judge evaluation, enabling continuous, scalable quality assurance. Overall, our work distinguishes itself from prior work by addressing the novel problem of business semantic layer induction from raw telemetry, operating without labeled training data or manual rule engineering.

</details>


### [93] [Improving Cross-Lingual Transfer for Sequential Sentence Classification in Research Papers via Structural Similarity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19650)
*Kazuhiro Yamauchi,Marie Katsurai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究多语言科学论文顺序句子分类中的跨语言迁移因素。作者构建覆盖13种非英语语言的数据集，比较编码器模型与生成式模型，发现语言亲近性不是稳定预测因素；修辞结构相似性具有弱而稳定的正相关，而控制源语言性能后，标签分布相似性是最一致的预测因素。基于此，作者提出三种利用结构信息的方法，生成式模型在域内达到与最佳编码器基线相当的性能，并在未见语言迁移中超过最佳编码器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 顺序句子分类有助于识别科学论文各句子的功能并组织科学文献，但非英语语言缺乏标注数据。跨语言迁移可以缓解数据稀缺问题。以往研究常强调源语言与目标语言的语言学亲近性，但顺序句子分类依赖篇章层面的修辞组织、标签序列和位置规律，这些规律可能跨语言共享。因此，本文旨在系统分析哪些因素决定多语言顺序句子分类的迁移效果，并探索如何显式利用结构信息提升迁移性能。

Method: 作者从五个学术数据库收集并构建覆盖13种非英语语言的多语言顺序句子分类数据集。实验采用编码器模型和生成式模型开展跨语言迁移，考察语言亲近性、修辞组织结构相似性、标签分布相似性等因素与迁移性能的关系；同时控制源语言任务性能，以区分数据和模型能力因素。在此基础上，作者为生成式模型设计了三种显式利用结构信息的方法，并分别进行域内评估和迁移到训练阶段未出现语言的评估。

Result: 实验表明，语言亲近性对迁移性能没有稳定的预测作用；修辞组织结构相似性与迁移性能存在弱但跨模型一致的正相关。控制源语言性能后，标签分布相似性是最稳定的迁移性能预测因素。利用结构信息的生成式方法中，最佳组合在域内任务上达到与最强编码器基线相当的性能，并在迁移至训练中未见语言时超过最强编码器基线。

Conclusion: 顺序句子分类的跨语言迁移成功与否，不能主要由语言学亲近性解释，篇章结构和标签分布等任务相关因素更为关键。显式建模这些结构信息能够增强生成式模型的跨语言泛化能力，尤其有利于未见语言迁移。本文说明，多语言SSC研究应从语言层面的相似性分析扩展到修辞组织和标签结构层面的分析。

Abstract: Sequential sentence classification (SSC) is an essential task for structuring scientific publications, and extending SSC research to languages other than English can improve accessibility to scientific knowledge in multilingual digital libraries. Cross-lingual transfer is a promising approach to address the scarcity of training data in non-English languages. Prior work on other natural language processing tasks has shown the benefits of capturing linguistic similarity between source and target languages. However, SSC inherently depends on patterns at the discourse level, such as label sequences and positional regularities, which appear consistently across languages regardless of linguistic differences. To examine the factors that determine transfer success in SSC, we constructed a multilingual SSC dataset covering 13 non-English languages collected from five academic databases. Our cross-lingual transfer experiments, using both encoder-based and generative models, show that linguistic proximity has no consistent predictive power for transfer performance, whereas structural similarity in rhetorical organization shows a weak but consistent positive correlation across models. After controlling for source-language performance, the similarity of label distributions is the most consistent predictor. Building on this finding, we propose a set of three methods that explicitly leverage structural information using generative models. In the in-domain evaluation, the best combination reaches parity with the strongest encoder baselines, and in transfer to languages unseen during training, it outperforms the strongest encoder baseline.

</details>


### [94] [A Phonemically Comprehensive, ASCII-Only Romanization Scheme for Thai and Lao: Systematic Cross-Lingual Correspondence and Chinese-User-Friendly Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19736)
*Zijie Zhang,Tan Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一套面向泰语和老挝语的、音位覆盖全面且仅使用ASCII字符的统一罗马化方案。该方案以一符号一音位为核心，编码辅音、元音、长短、词汇声调，并兼顾跨语言对应、可读性、键盘输入便利性及机器处理需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有泰语和老挝语罗马化方案可能在音位透明度、声调表示、跨语言一致性、输入便利性或机器可处理性方面存在不足。由于两种语言关系密切，本文将其视为统一的跨语言设计问题，旨在建立一种既能准确表达音系对立，又便于学习、输入和语音处理的表示系统。

Method: 作者设计一套仅由ASCII字符构成的音位罗马化体系，采用一符号一音位原则表示辅音和元音对立，并明确编码元音长度与词汇声调。方案优先考虑共时语音对应关系，在适用情况下与汉语拼音和粤拼建立对应；若不损害语音透明度，则保留历史音系对应。声调方面采用紧凑的单数字默认标记，同时提供可选的实际调值表示和历史声调类别表示。

Result: 论文形成了一套覆盖较全面的泰语—老挝语音位对立的统一罗马化方案，能够系统表示音段、元音长短和词汇声调，并保持两种语言之间的对应关系。该方案具有可读、易于键盘输入和适合机器处理等特点，也可支持语言学习及跨语言语音处理。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献是提出一种兼顾音系准确性、跨语言一致性和实际可用性的泰语与老挝语ASCII罗马化方案。通过将默认声调数字、可选声调值及历史声调类别结合起来，该方案在简洁性与信息完整性之间取得平衡，具有作为学习工具和计算语言学表示标准的潜力。

Abstract: This paper proposes a phonemically comprehensive, ASCII-only romanization scheme for Thai and Lao, treating the two closely related languages as a unified cross-lingual design problem. The scheme represents segmental contrasts, vowel length, and lexical tone while maintaining one-symbol-one-phoneme transparency and systematic correspondence between Thai and Lao.
