We hypothesise that this difficulty arises not only from comparing two contextual uses of a word, but also from the absence of an explicit sense inventory that specifies the relevant level of semantic granularity. We evaluate open LLMs on WiC and traditional Word Sense Disambiguation (WSD) under similar settings. We find that providing candidate senses, similar to what is done in traditional WSD, improves WiC performance in all settings. In general, explicit sense information helps models make more consistent and targeted judgements. Human evaluation further shows that many apparent WiC errors reflect label ambiguity or mismatches between model and annotator sense boundaries rather than simple failures of lexical understanding. In particular, results show that LLMs overthink the sense distinction often leading to errors based on overly fine-grained distinctions.

</details>


### [128] [What Does Privileged Information Add to On-Policy Self-Distillation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20612)
*XiuYu Zhang,Wei Chow,Junfeng Fang,Zhenkai Liang,Tat-Seng Chua*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文研究在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）中，教师额外获得答案或完整解题过程所带来的真实增益。作者通过构建具有相同答案、不同推理视图的 AMPLE-Math，并与匹配的无参考蒸馏进行对照，发现大部分提升来自蒸馏本身及不同推理模式之间的能力迁移，而非参考答案信息本身。参考信息的额外价值有限、依赖学生模型和训练设置，且更长的思维轨迹不一定更有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPSD中教师可访问答案或完整解法，但学生性能提升究竟来自蒸馏机制，还是来自教师额外看到的参考信息，尚不清楚。论文旨在隔离并量化参考信息的独立贡献，同时解释不同学生模型、推理模式和监督信号为何产生不同结果。

Method: 构建AMPLE-Math数据集，包含5,319道数学题及六种共享同一答案的推理视图；将不同视图与匹配的无参考蒸馏进行比较。实验覆盖Qwen3-1.7B和SmolLM3-3B，比较直接回答与思维启用式rollout、简短与完整解题轨迹，并通过教师画像和匹配的损失干预分析token级监督变化与学生行为的关系。

Result: 在思维启用式评测下，无参考蒸馏已解释Qwen3-1.7B大部分提升，包括域内和外部基准。参考信息的额外收益在Qwen中较小，但经过润色的解法最明显；在SmolLM3-3B中，完整解题轨迹到第50步可带来约两个百分点的额外收益。收益取决于学生模型；在相同检查点下，将短直接回答rollout替换为长思维rollout，两个模型系列的收益反而转为损失。教师画像和损失干预表明，改变token级监督未必显著改变学生行为。

Conclusion: OPSD的主要作用可能是通过参数中共享的能力，在直接回答和思维启用推理之间实现跨模式迁移，从而释放模型已有的推理能力。参考答案的价值不在于展示了多少解题内容，而在于其是否促进了这种跨模式迁移；这一价值具有模型和训练设置依赖性，不能简单地将更长或更完整的参考轨迹视为更优监督。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) lets a language model learn from a frozen copy of itself that sees an answer or a worked solution. Giving the teacher this extra information seems to offer the student more to learn, but how much does it add beyond distillation itself? To isolate that contribution, we construct AMPLE-Math, a reusable suite of 5,319 mathematical problems with six reasoning views that share the same answer, and compare each view with matched reference-free distillation. With a thinking-enabled teacher supervising direct-response rollouts, reference-free distillation accounts for much of Qwen3-1.7B's improvement under thinking-enabled evaluation, both in domain and on external benchmarks. Evidence for an additional reference benefit is modest in Qwen, strongest for a polished solution, whereas complete traces add two percentage points in SmolLM3-3B at step 50. These benefits depend on the student being trained. At the same checkpoint, replacing short direct-response rollouts with long thinking-enabled rollouts turns gains into losses in both families while the problems, references, and evaluation stay fixed. Teacher profiles and matched loss interventions in Qwen further show that changing token-level supervision can leave student behavior largely unchanged. Together, these findings suggest that OPSD can improve access to existing reasoning capabilities through parameters shared by direct-response and thinking-enabled inference. The value of a privileged reference is what it adds to this cross-mode transfer, not how much of the solution it reveals.

</details>


### [129] [Chronicle: Cut-Point Replay for Regression Testing of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20625)
*Tisha Chawla,Susheem Koul*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 Chronicle，一种面向大语言模型智能体的记录—回放测试框架。它在非确定性边界记录不可变数据包，并通过切点回放选择性地回放部分边界、对其余边界执行新代码，从而将历史故障转化为可在持续集成中运行的回归测试。实验表明，该方法开销极低、回放稳定，并显著优于将所有边界替换为桩的基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型响应具有非确定性，智能体还会依赖不断变化的工具状态并执行多步轨迹，因此故障难以稳定复现。现有记录—回放工具主要用于追踪或评分，不能有效用于验证代码修改。研究需要一种能够保留真实故障上下文、同时让修改后的代码接受可重复测试的机制。

Method: Chronicle在智能体运行的非确定性边界记录不可变的 envelopes，并依据记录进行回放。其核心是切点回放：对选定边界使用历史记录，对互补边界使用新代码实时执行，以隔离并测试特定代码变化。作者在包含6个已记录故障的基准上评估记录开销、完整回放稳定性、切点测试的回归检测能力，并通过受保护工具的突变实验与“所有边界均桩化”的基线进行比较。

Result: 记录开销为每次边界穿越23微秒，相对于假设的300毫秒模型调用仅为0.008%。完整回放不产生模型调用，并在20次重复中保持位级稳定。对于全部6个故障，切点测试均能使错误代码测试失败，并使受保护代码和无害修改通过。在突变实验中，切点测试捕获了所有会放行原有不安全操作的突变，而使用相同断言、但将所有边界桩化的基线一个也未捕获。

Conclusion: Chronicle证明了在非确定性、多步骤的LLM智能体系统中，将记录—回放机制用于回归测试是可行且高效的。切点回放既保留了真实运行中的关键上下文，又允许新代码在选定边界上真实执行，因此比全量桩化更能发现安全性和行为回归问题。该基准和实现已公开，可支持后续复现与研究。

Abstract: Large language model responses are non-deterministic, so failures in LLM agents are hard to reproduce: a failure depends on inference that is not bitwise reproducible, on tools that read changing state, and on a multi-step trajectory that a re-run rarely repeats. Record-and-replay makes a run reproducible, but existing agent tooling records runs only to trace or score them, not to test a code change against them. We present Chronicle, which records an agent run at its non-deterministic boundaries as immutable envelopes and replays it from the record. Its central operation, cut-point replay, serves a chosen subset of boundaries from the record and executes the complementary subset live with new code, turning a recorded incident into a regression test that runs in continuous integration. On a benchmark of 6 recorded failures with simulated model boundaries, recording adds 23 μs per crossing (0.008% of an assumed 300 ms model call), full replay issues zero model calls and is bit-stable across 20 repetitions, and cut-point tests fail on faulty code and pass on guarded and benign changes for all 6 incidents. In a mutation study of the guarded tools, cut-point tests catch every mutant that lets the recorded unsafe action through, while a baseline that stubs every boundary, using the same assertion, catches none. Chronicle and the benchmark are publicly available at https://github.com/theagentplane/chronicle.

</details>


### [130] [UniPolicy: Unified Objective-Specific Policies for Generative Search Advertising](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20630)
*Kun Yao,Yuhang Zhou,Yichi Zhang,Zeliang Tong,Shengri Xue,Haitao Wang,Siyu Lu,Qianlong Xie,Xingxing Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: UniPolicy 是一种面向搜索广告多目标对齐的多策略框架，通过分层参数解耦、行为反馈偏好建模和多策略束搜索，在相关性、点击率与商业价值之间取得平衡。离线实验优于单目标强化学习和朴素奖励融合；线上 7 天 A/B 测试中 CTR 提升 0.71%、RPS 提升 1.58%、广告收入提升 1.32%，且服务延迟稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有搜索广告生成系统通常围绕单一商业目标（如 eCPM）进行对齐，或采用简单的奖励融合，难以同时优化相关性、点击倾向和商业价值。多目标联合训练还容易产生梯度竞争，导致整体性能陷入全局次优，并可能损害用户体验或检索质量。因此，需要一种能够区分不同业务目标、缓解参数干扰并支持灵活业务权衡的统一策略对齐方法。

Method: 提出 UniPolicy objective-aware multi-policy alignment 框架。第一，使用目标专属前缀 token、稀疏 MoE-LoRA 路由和目标专属残差 FFN，在共享骨干网络中进行分层参数解耦，为不同业务目标提供差异化的参数空间和策略表达空间。第二，从多阶段行为反馈构造成对偏好数据，补充曝光但未点击样本中的相对偏好信息，并增强点击候选在生成分布中的相对优势。第三，在推理阶段采用并行、可按业务定制的多策略 beam search，在固定检索预算下灵活分配不同目标的候选配额。

Result: 大规模离线实验表明，UniPolicy 在多个评价指标上实现较为均衡的提升，同时保持检索质量，并优于单目标强化学习和朴素奖励融合基线。真实搜索广告系统的 7 天线上 A/B 测试显示：CTR 提升 0.71%，RPS 提升 1.58%，广告收入提升 1.32%；服务延迟保持稳定。

Conclusion: UniPolicy 通过将多目标策略在参数、偏好学习和推理阶段进行协同解耦，有效缓解了多目标优化中的梯度竞争和目标冲突，在用户体验与商业收益之间实现了更好的平衡。结果说明，目标感知的多策略对齐和可配置候选配额机制适用于实际搜索广告系统，并具备线上部署价值。

Abstract: Search advertising connects user intent with commercial content and plays a critical role in platform monetization. Recent systems typically align pretrained generative models with a single business reward, such as eCPM, or use naive reward fusion for preliminary multi-objective alignment. However, an ideal search advertising system must jointly account for heterogeneous objectives, including relevance, click propensity, and commercial value, to balance user experience and business value while mitigating globally suboptimal performance caused by gradient competition. We propose UniPolicy, an objective-aware multi-policy alignment framework. UniPolicy combines objective-specific prefix tokens, sparse MoE-LoRA routing, and objective-specific residual FFNs to hierarchically decouple parameters within a shared backbone, providing differentiated parameter and policy-expression spaces for different business objectives. It further constructs pairwise preferences from multi-stage behavioral feedback, supplementing the relative preference information in exposed-but-unclicked samples and strengthening the relative advantage of clicked candidates in the generation distribution. At inference, UniPolicy supports parallel, business-customizable multi-policy beam search, flexibly allocating candidate quotas across objectives under a fixed retrieval budget. Large-scale offline experiments show that UniPolicy delivers balanced improvements across multiple metrics while preserving retrieval quality, outperforming single-objective reinforcement learning and naive reward-fusion baselines. In a 7-day online A/B test on a real search advertising system, UniPolicy improves CTR by 0.71%, RPS by 1.58%, and advertising revenue by 1.32%, while maintaining stable serving latency.

</details>


### [131] [HerHealthEval: Evaluating Multilingual and Register-Sensitive Understanding of Women's Health Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20684)
*Hassan Saeed Hassan Albattra,Mazen Mohammed Bahgat,Rahatara Ferdousi,Hana Essam Sayed Ahmed Amrya,Mariam Mousa*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 HerHealthEval，多语言、多表达形式地评估医疗沟通模型是否正确理解用户的健康担忧，并重点检验风险校准、澄清行为及跨语言一致性。结果表明，整体准确率可能掩盖严重的安全风险；语言不对称的风险监督会导致法语和阿拉伯语中的严重漏分诊，而采用来源可靠且语言不变的风险标签可显著改善这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医疗大语言模型评估通常侧重回答质量，默认模型已经正确理解用户意图，因而忽视了表达形式、语言差异、不确定信息和澄清需求对安全性的影响。本文旨在建立更严格的多语言理解评估框架，识别聚合指标掩盖的风险。

Method: 构建 HerHealthEval：针对每个临床案例，生成英语、法语和现代标准阿拉伯语版本，并覆盖六种沟通形式：规范表达、临床表达、通俗表达、间接或含蓄表达、情绪化担忧表达，以及故意省略关键信息的欠明确表达。前五种形式保持相同临床信息，欠明确形式用于测试模型是否会主动要求澄清。评估基础多语言指令模型及 QLoRA 适配模型，指标包括担忧分类、风险校准、澄清行为、格式遵循和跨表达形式一致性；同时比较不同风险标签监督策略。

Result: 结果显示，整体准确率和一致性并不能充分反映安全性。采用语言不对称风险监督的多语言适配模型，在法语和阿拉伯语中的漏分诊率分别达到 0.994。使用源数据衍生且语言不变的风险标签重新适配后，漏分诊率分别降至 0.572 和 0.558，说明监督标签的语言一致性与来源质量对模型安全表现具有关键影响。

Conclusion: 多语言医疗模型评估应显式覆盖不同语言和语域、信息不完整情形及不确定性处理，不能仅依赖平均准确率或一致性指标。模型适配还必须审查风险标签的来源、定义和跨语言不变性，以减少语言特定监督造成的系统性安全失误。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used in healthcare communication, yet most evaluations emphasize response quality while assuming that the user's concern has been interpreted correctly. We introduce HerHealthEval, a controlled evaluation framework for multilingual understanding of women's-health communication. For each clinical case, HerHealthEval provides matched versions in English, French, and Modern Standard Arabic using six communicative forms: canonical, clinical, layperson, indirect or hedged, emotionally concerned, and deliberately under-specified. The first five express the same underlying concern and retain the same clinical information, whereas the under-specified form intentionally omits relevant details to test whether the model recognizes that clarification is needed. We evaluate a multilingual instruction model and QLoRA-adapted variants on concern classification, risk calibration, clarification behavior, parse compliance, and cross-form consistency. Results reveal that aggregate accuracy and consistency can conceal safety-relevant failures. A multilingual adaptation model reaches 0.994 under-triage in French and Arabic under language-asymmetric risk supervision. A controlled re-adaptation using source-derived, language-invariant risk labels reduces under-triage to 0.572 and 0.558, respectively. These findings show that robust multilingual healthcare evaluation requires explicit testing of register variation, uncertainty handling, and the provenance and invariance of adaptation labels.

</details>


### [132] [Summarization Bias: The Directional Collapse of Objective Projection into Told-Mode Labels in Large Language Models --- A Conceptual Framework and Registered Test Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20712)
*Levent Bulut*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出并操作化“总结偏差”（summarization bias）：大型语言模型可能倾向于把叙事意义压缩为抽象的情绪或意义标签，而不是重建产生该意义的推理结构。论文区分生成性偏差与评价性偏差，并重点关注后者对文学文本选择机制的潜在影响；目前仅提出构念、提供一致性线索并预注册检验方案，尚未声称完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于大语言模型作为评价者和奖励模型的偏差研究，较少专门考察模型是否系统性偏好“直白告知”而低估“通过物理线索和间接表达让读者自行推断”的展示式叙事。该问题具有实际重要性：如果模型评价偏向显性表达，模型生成、筛选和奖励机制可能共同推动文本趋向平直、解释性更强但重建负荷更低的风格。

Method: 论文首先在Bulut Doctrine框架下定义“告知—展示”轴：告知模式直接声明情绪和信息，展示模式则压低表层表达，要求读者依据物理线索和间接性进行重建。随后提出两个待检验机制：生成性机制测试模型在要求通过客观投射呈现情绪时是否改为直接声明；评价性机制测试模型是否偏好告知式文本、低估展示式文本的叙事质量。论文还重新解读一项已完成的独立可靠性研究，将其作为方向一致但非决定性的证据，并预注册双机制实验及明确的放弃该构念的判定规则。

Result: 目前没有报告对总结偏差的正式验证结果。论文仅指出既有独立可靠性研究提供了与该假设方向一致的初步线索，并提出后续实验设计。因此，现阶段结论应被视为理论构念和可证伪研究计划，而非已证实的模型偏差。

Conclusion: 论文的核心贡献是提出一个具体、具有方向性的偏差假设，并将其转化为可操作、可证伪的双机制检验框架。其最重要的风险判断是：评价性偏差可能通过评审模型和奖励模型形成选择压力，使叙事从展示式结构退化为告知式陈述。由于当前证据尚不足，论文保持谨慎立场，并明确承诺在预注册决策规则不支持时放弃该构念。

Abstract: This paper introduces and operationalizes summarization bias: a proposed systematic tendency of large language models (LLMs) to represent narrative meaning as an abstract summary label rather than as the reconstructable inferential structure that produces it. Within the Bulut Doctrine, narrative effect is theorized along a told-shown axis: in told mode, emotional and informational content is declared explicitly and requires little reader reconstruction; in shown mode, that content is suppressed at the surface and must be reconstructed from physical cues and indirection (Objective Projection). Shown mode is the higher-load condition the doctrine is designed to measure.
  The claim is that LLMs fail along this axis in a specific direction. Summarization bias is hypothesized to operate in two regimes: (i) a generative regime, in which a model asked to render an emotion through Objective Projection defaults to declaring it instead; and (ii) an evaluative regime, in which a model judging narrative quality rewards told-mode explicitness and under-detects shown-mode suppression. The evaluative regime is the more consequential, since LLMs increasingly serve as judges and reward models, and a directional bias toward told mode would impose a selection pressure degrading prose toward flat declaration.
  This report does not claim the bias is validated. It defines the construct, situates it against LLM-as-judge biases, rereads a completed independent reliability study as directional evidence consistent with it, and pre-registers a two-regime test with decision rules under which the construct would be abandoned.

</details>


### [133] [On-Demand Attention: Language Models Know When to Recall](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20734)
*Haibo Feng,Ruiqi Liang,Hanyang Peng,Shiqi Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出 On-Demand Attention（ODA），利用预训练模型解码状态预测全局历史信息对下一步预测的价值，仅在必要时触发全局注意力，从而减少长上下文推理中的全局 KV 读取，并在 vLLM 中实现实际加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文生成时，标准全注意力解码每一步都会读取不断增长的全部历史 KV 缓存，即使大部分历史对当前预测没有帮助，导致显著的访存和推理延迟开销。局部注意力虽然高效，但可能丢失远距离关键信息。因此，论文希望让模型动态判断何时需要访问全局历史，在效率与性能之间取得更好的平衡。

Method: 提出 On-Demand Attention（ODA）局部优先的解码机制：先基于局部上下文进行解码，并使用轻量级 recall head 根据全局读取前的预训练模型解码状态，预测全局历史信息对当前预测的潜在收益；当预测收益发生变化或达到触发条件时，再选择性调用全局注意力。训练时仅更新 recall head，冻结原有预训练模型权重，同时保留完整历史 KV cache，以支持未来的全局召回。系统层面在 vLLM 中实现 GPU 端条件执行，将减少的全局读取转化为实际解码加速。

Result: 在 Qwen、Gemma 以及包含混合注意力结构的模型上，ODA 能够显著减少全局注意力读取，并恢复局部注意力造成的大部分性能损失。在长上下文场景下，相较全注意力解码取得了实际推理速度提升，说明模型自身的解码状态可以有效指导是否访问历史信息。摘要未提供具体的准确率、召回率、读取比例或加速倍数。

Conclusion: 论文表明，预训练模型的解码状态在执行全局读取之前，已经包含预测全局历史价值的有效信号。通过轻量级选择性召回，长上下文推理可以避免不必要的全局 KV 读取，同时保持接近全注意力的任务性能。该工作支持一种由模型自主决定信息访问范围的高效长上下文推理范式。

Abstract: Reasoning and agentic workloads increasingly demand efficient long-context inference. Yet full-attention decoding reads the growing history at every step, regardless of its benefit to the next prediction. We show that a pretrained model's decoding states already contain information predictive of this benefit, before the global read. Building on this finding, we introduce On-Demand Attention (ODA), a local-first decoding method that uses a lightweight recall head to selectively invoke global attention as its predicted benefit changes during generation. ODA trains only the recall head, leaving pretrained weights unchanged and the complete historical KV cache available for future recall. We further implement GPU-side conditional execution in vLLM, translating reduced global reads into practical decoding speedups over full attention at long context lengths. Experiments across Qwen and Gemma models, including hybrid-attention backbones, show that selective recall recovers most of the performance lost under local attention while substantially reducing global reads. These findings support long-context inference in which pretrained models guide their own access to the information they retain.

</details>


### [134] [dQwen3.5: Hybrid-Attention Diffusion Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20751)
*Anton Xue,Litu Rout,Aditya Akella,Adam Klivans,Sujay Sanghavi,Sanjay Shakkottai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文研究将混合注意力-RNN自回归模型适配为扩散语言模型，提出dQwen3.5系列，并发现其训练效率和并行解码表现优于预期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有从预训练自回归模型适配扩散语言模型的方法几乎都依赖全注意力Transformer。随着自回归建模转向交替使用注意力与RNN层的混合架构，RNN的结构性因果约束使其难以直接双向化，因此需要验证这类混合骨干能否有效适配为扩散语言模型。

Method: 以Qwen3.5的0.8B、2B、4B和9B规模模型为基础进行扩散语言模型适配，构建dQwen3.5系列，并与全注意力架构控制组比较训练损失达到速度、任意顺序解码行为以及并行解码性能。

Result: 混合架构能够成为高效的扩散语言模型适配起点；与全注意力控制组相比，达到相同训练损失约只需一半训练token。不同规模的dQwen3.5在任意顺序解码行为上接近全注意力扩散语言模型，并在并行解码任务中表现较强。

Conclusion: 混合注意力-RNN骨干虽然具有因果性和双向化困难，但仍可有效适配为扩散语言模型。dQwen3.5证明了这类架构不仅训练效率高，而且能够支持接近全注意力模型的任意顺序与并行解码能力。

Abstract: Adapting a pretrained autoregressive (AR) model is a cost-efficient route to a diffusion language model (DLM). While nearly all such adaptations start from a full-attention transformer, AR modeling has shifted toward hybrid architectures that interleave attention and RNN layers. This creates an obstacle for adaptation: unlike attention, RNNs are structurally causal and nontrivial to bidirectionalize. Despite this mismatch, we investigate whether such backbones can become effective DLMs by adapting Qwen3.5 at 0.8B, 2B, 4B, and 9B scales, yielding the dQwen3.5 family. We find that hybrid backbones can be efficient starting points for adaptation: against a full-attention control, the hybrid reaches a given training loss in about half the tokens. Across scales, dQwen3.5 resembles full-attention DLMs in any-order decoding behavior and performs strongly under parallel decoding.

</details>


### [135] [Harm Laundering in GPT Models: Evidence That Gender Discrimination Is Transformed Rather Than Reduced Across Safety-Trained Generations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20779)
*Sarah Wyer,Sue Black,Noura Al Moubayed*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该摘要研究大型语言模型中的“伤害洗白”现象，指出表面毒性下降可能掩盖了歧视内容的重构与表征性伤害转移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全评估主要依赖表面毒性分类器，可能将显性有害内容的减少误判为整体伤害减少，因此需要检验潜在的表征性不平等。

Method: 分析15个GPT系模型、约45万条按性别条件生成的文本，结合主题聚类、情感分析、主题多样性、REGARD与Detoxify等指标，并提出三标准判定法和三阶段检测流程。

Result: 研究声称，较新模型中显性有害内容减少，但性别条件之间的主题覆盖、情感倾向和表征性伤害出现新的不对称；REGARD指标与发布时间相关，而Detoxify未显示类似关系。

Conclusion: 毒性分数下降不能单独作为伤害减少的充分证据，生成模型安全评估应同时考察潜在的表征性伤害与内容重构。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for large language models rely on surface-form classifiers that report declining harm scores across model generations. We provide evidence that this methodology is systematically incomplete: explicit discriminatory content is transformed rather than removed. We call this \emph{harm laundering}. Analysing 450,000 gender-directed completions across 15 models spanning GPT-2 through to GPT-5 (OpenAI GPT lineage; three demographic conditions), we show that sexual violence clusters prevalent in GPT-2 women-directed output disappear by GPT-4, while men-directed completions gain positive representational territory (caregiving, emotional range, ally identity) that women-directed completions do not. The pattern is most visible at GPT-5: Topic~5 (1,997~documents) frames breast cancer as a men's rights debate, while zero equivalent clusters appear in women-directed output. Three independent classifiers score this content as non-toxic. Sentiment scores invert at GPT-4: early models demean women; later models over-correct. Topic diversity in women-directed completions falls 36\% relative to men at the GPT-4 alignment boundary (W/M~$= 0.58$, from $0.91$ at GPT-2). REGARD representational harm disparity correlates with release date ($ρ= +0.55$, $p = .034$) while Detoxify does not ($ρ= -0.23$, $p = .42$): toxicity scores fall as representational harm grows. We formalise harm laundering as a three-criteria test and provide a three-stage detection protocol applicable to any generative model. Within the OpenAI GPT lineage, toxicity score reduction is not a sufficient proxy for harm reduction.

</details>


### [136] [RetireOPD: Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20784)
*Yan Yu,Zhengxi Lu,Yizhou Liu,Yichen Pan,Aozhe Wang,Qipeng Chen,Hua Yang,Wenqi Zhang,Weiming Lu,Qianglong Chen,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RetireOPD是一种自适应自蒸馏方法：先训练具备技能条件的教师，再让无技能学生结合强化学习与在线策略蒸馏训练，并在学生达到教师一定成功率且差异不再缩小时自动退出教师监督。该方法在ALFWorld和WebShop上均显著优于RL基线，并超过教师模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轮智能体强化学习通常每条轨迹只有一个标量奖励，监督稀疏，在线策略蒸馏可通过教师提供密集的token级监督来缓解这一问题。然而，现有方法存在两个局限：仅有特权信息并不能保证教师始终可靠；教师监督的收益具有阶段依赖性，固定的蒸馏调度可能在后期限制学生继续通过强化学习优化。因此，需要一种能判断教师何时应退出的自适应训练机制。

Method: 提出RetireOPD（Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation）。方法分两阶段：首先利用环境奖励训练一个解耦的、技能条件化教师；随后训练不具备技能先验的学生，使其联合使用强化学习目标和在线策略蒸馏（OPD）的token级监督。核心是Adaptive Retirement：不预先设定蒸馏停止时间，而是当学生与教师之间的差异不再缩小，且学生达到教师成功率的目标比例时，自动移除教师监督，后续仅使用强化学习继续训练。

Result: 在Qwen2.5系列1.5B至7B模型上，RetireOPD相较RL基线在ALFWorld上的成功率提升14.1%至18.8%，在WebShop上的准确率提升11.8%至19.0%。在所有测试设置中，RetireOPD还超过了其自身的技能条件化教师。

Conclusion: 结果表明，教师蒸馏应当根据训练阶段动态调整，而非采用固定计划。RetireOPD通过让学生在适当时机自主摆脱教师监督，兼顾了早期的密集指导与后期的独立强化学习优化，能够有效提升多轮智能体任务性能。

Abstract: Multi-turn agents trained with reinforcement learning (RL) receive a single scalar reward per trajectory, which motivates self on-policy distillation (OPD) to supply dense token-level supervision from a self-teacher with privileged task skills, letting a skill-free student internalize them. This recipe, however, is undermined by two findings in agentic tasks: privileged information alone does not always make a teacher reliable, and the benefit of teacher supervision is stage-dependent. We therefore propose RetireOPD (Self-Retiring On-Policy Distillation), which first optimizes a decoupled, skill-conditioned teacher with environment rewards and then trains a skill-free student jointly with RL and OPD. Rather than following a predefined distillation schedule, RetireOPD adopts Adaptive Retirement: the student drops the teacher on its own once their discrepancy stops shrinking and it reaches a target fraction of the teacher's success rate, after which training proceeds with RL alone. Across Qwen2.5 models from 1.5B to 7B, RetireOPD improves ALFWorld success rate over RL baseline by 14.1% to 18.8% and WebShop accuracy by 11.8% to 19.0%, and surpasses its own skill-conditioned teacher in every setting.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [137] [Polynomial-Time MIMO Detection at the Maximum-Likelihood Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19405)
*Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文证明：在方形高斯二元MIMO模型中，舍入LMMSE结合单比特最陡下降法，可在O(N^3)时间内实现精确块恢复，其一阶SNR阈值与ML检测相同，约为ρ=2 log N；同时证明低于约2 log N的带宽时，即使ML也无法可靠恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AMP状态演化和复制/MMSE理论能够刻画固定参数下的归一化性能，但不能直接给出维度相关区域中的全坐标、零错误恢复保证。已有多项式时间方法（如盒约束松弛）的阈值约为4 log N，明显高于ML的一阶阈值。因此，论文旨在证明多项式时间算法能否达到ML精确恢复边界，并刻画统计极限与计算极限是否一致。

Method: 模型为 y=sqrt(ρ/N)Hx⋆+w，其中x⋆∈{±1}^N，H和w为独立标准高斯变量。算法先计算线性MMSE估计并进行符号舍入，得到与真实向量仅有次线性汉明距离的初始解；随后执行单比特最陡下降，每次翻转能带来目标函数下降的错误比特。证明中对算法可能访问的所有错误集合建立统一的局部下降性质，并利用目标函数屏障限制搜索路径，从而保证最终到达真实向量。下界部分通过构造一比特邻居证明，当ρ≤2 log N−log log N−s_N（s_N→∞且s_N=o(log N)）时，错误邻居以高概率优于真实向量，因此ML也失败。

Result: 当ρ≥2 log N时，算法对任意发送字x⋆都以趋近于1的概率精确恢复，计算复杂度为O(N^3)次单位成本精确实数运算。该阈值与ML检测的一阶阈值一致。相比之下，先前方形系统的盒约束松弛一阶阈值为4 log N。低SNR方面，当ρ≤2 log N−log log N−s_N时，即使ML检测也以趋近于1的概率失败。

Conclusion: 在给定的精确实数算术模型下，该问题的统计精确恢复阈值与多项式时间精确恢复阈值在一阶意义上重合，均位于2 log N尺度。论文的核心贡献是将固定参数性能理论推进到维度相关的全坐标精确恢复，并证明简单的舍入LMMSE加局部搜索即可达到ML边界。

Abstract: We prove that exact block recovery in the square Gaussian binary MIMO model can be achieved in polynomial time at the same first-order SNR threshold as exhaustive maximum-likelihood detection. Specifically, for $y=\sqrt{ρ/N}Hx^\star+w, \; x^\star\in\{\pm1\}^N,$ and independent standard Gaussian $H\in\mathbb R^{N\times N}$ and $w$, rounded linear MMSE followed by steepest single-bit descent recovers $x^\star$ with failure probability tending to zero, uniformly over every transmitted word and every $ρ\ge2\log N$, using $O(N^3)$ unit-cost exact-real arithmetic operations. The model is a special case of Gaussian random linear estimation, for which AMP state evolution and replica/MMSE formulas rigorously characterize fixed-parameter normalized performance. Those results predict the same $2\log N$ scale, but do not by themselves yield an all-coordinate guarantee in the dimension-dependent regime considered here. To the best of our knowledge, no prior work gives polynomial-time exact block recovery at the ML boundary for this setting; the closest prior square-system theorem, for the box relaxation, has first-order threshold $4\log N$. The proof places the rounded LMMSE estimate at sublinear Hamming distance from the truth, and then establishes, uniformly over every error set the local search can visit, that some wrong bit offers a quantified cost decrease while an objective barrier confines the search path. Conversely, if $0<ρ\le2\log N-\log\log N-s_N$ with $s_N\to\infty$ and $s_N=o(\log N)$, then a one-bit neighbor beats the transmitted word with probability tending to one, so even ML detection fails. Therefore, the statistical and polynomial-time exact-recovery thresholds coincide to first order in the stated arithmetic model.

</details>


### [138] [Binary Deletion Channel Capacity to Within One Hundredth of a Bit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19412)
*Dimitris Papailiopoulos*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对二元删除信道容量未知的问题，提出经计算机辅助验证的全参数范围容量近似：对任意删除概率，误差低于0.0095 bit/bit，平均认证误差低于0.006522；同时给出接近容量的可实现速率、严格上下界、数值验证和可复现程序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二元删除信道的精确容量长期未被确定。现有研究虽已积累大量可实现速率与反演界，但缺乏在所有删除概率上同时具有严格误差保证的高精度容量估计，因此需要一种可计算、可认证且可复现的方法。

Method: 上界方面，将问题约化到平稳输入源，并建立覆盖所有允许输入配置的有限不等式；通过两种有限窗口外控制构造处理未观测输入：一种使用共同的外部幸存序列并界定遗漏删除模式，另一种抵消熵项，使外部概率以线性形式出现。下界方面，采用有限状态输入，结合输出熵估计与相容删除掩码的特定不相交计数；同时研究独立游程输入，以保留输出游程边界的不确定性。通过定向数值检验验证有限不等式，再利用删除概率之间的解析比较，将点态结果扩展到整个参数区间。

Result: 获得对所有删除概率均成立的容量近似，认证误差小于0.0095 bit/bit；删除概率均匀加权下的平均认证误差小于0.006522。估计值取显式上下界的中点。下界对应的渐近编码可实现速率与信道容量之差小于0.019 bit。论文还报告计算成本，并提供完整数值输入和程序以支持复核。

Conclusion: 该工作将二元删除信道容量的未知问题转化为高精度、全参数、可计算验证的近似问题，显著收紧了容量上下界，并通过完整的推导与开源式复现材料增强了结果的可信度和可验证性。

Abstract: The exact capacity of the binary deletion channel remains unknown despite decades of work on achievable rates and converse bounds. We establish a computer-assisted approximation whose error is below $0.0095$ bits per transmitted bit, uniformly over all deletion probabilities. The mean certified error bound, with uniform weighting of deletion probability, is below $0.006522$. The estimate is the midpoint of explicit lower and upper bounds. For the converse, a stationary-source reduction is combined with finite inequalities covering every allowed input configuration. Two constructions control the unobserved input beyond a finite window: one uses a common outside survivor sequence and bounds omitted deletion patterns, while the other cancels an entropy term to make outside probabilities enter linearly. For the lower bound, finite-state inputs combine output-entropy estimates with selected disjoint counts of compatible deletion masks; independent-run inputs retain additional uncertainty about output-run boundaries. Directed numerical checks establish the finite inequalities. An analytic comparison between deletion probabilities then extends the pointwise bounds over the entire parameter range. The lower endpoint supplies rates within $0.019$ bits of capacity in the asymptotic coding sense. We give the derivations, recorded computational costs, and complete numerical inputs and programs needed to verify the result.

</details>


### [139] [Source Entropy-Guided Adaptive Transmission for Communication-Driven Multi-View Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19457)
*Mingjie Yang,Guangming Liang,Dongzhu Liu,Lei Zhang,Xiaonan Liu,Kaibin Huang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种源熵引导的自适应传输框架，联合优化通信驱动的多视角感知、数据上传与边缘推理，在有限比特预算和时变信道下提升CSI手势识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通信驱动的多视角感知存在感知采集与边缘推理的耦合：通信间隔决定可获得的源信息，而上行信道条件决定能够上传至边缘服务器的信息量。有限通信资源下，需要根据源信息熵和可用传输预算，自适应选择原始数据传输或面向任务的传输，以提高推理准确率。

Method: 首先，利用多输出高斯过程刻画由数据包触发的信道状态信息（CSI）熵，并将通信间隔与源熵联系起来。随后，将该解析熵界与由传输速率和时延要求决定的比特预算进行比较，据此选择原始数据传输或面向任务的传输。对于面向任务的传输，基于信息瓶颈建立通信约束下的推理问题，并将其分解为自适应分布式编码和多视角推理（ADE-MI），从而避免设备端与边缘服务器之间的交替优化。

Result: 在 Widar3.0 多视角 CSI 手势识别数据集上的实验表明：所提出的解析熵界能够紧密跟随基于归一化流的数值估计；在相同比特预算下，ADE-MI 优于已有面向任务的传输基准；在时变信道环境中，完整的源熵引导自适应框架能够进一步提升识别准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究通过显式建模源熵、通信预算与推理性能之间的关系，实现了感知采集、数据传输和边缘推理的联合自适应。结果说明，基于源信息熵动态选择传输模式，并采用解耦的ADE-MI进行任务导向传输，能够有效应对有限资源和时变信道。

Abstract: Communication-driven multi-view sensing relies on routine communication transmissions for sensing acquisition, while the resulting sensing data at distributed devices must be uploaded to an edge server under limited communication resources. This creates a unique coupling between sensing acquisition and edge inference: the communication interval determines the source information, whereas the uplink condition determines how much information can be delivered to the server for sensing inference. To account for this coupling, we propose a source entropy-guided adaptive transmission framework. Specifically, we characterize the entropy of packet-triggered channel state information (CSI) as a function of the communication interval using a multi-output Gaussian process. The resulting analytical bound is compared with the available bit budget, determined by the transmission rate and latency requirement, to select between original-data and task-oriented transmission. For task-oriented transmission, we formulate the communication-constrained inference problem based on the information bottleneck and decompose it into adaptive distributed encoding and multi-view inference (ADE-MI), which avoids alternating optimization between the devices and the edge server. Experiments on the Widar3.0 multi-view CSI gesture recognition dataset show that the analytical bound closely follows the normalizing-flow numerical estimate, while ADE-MI outperforms task-oriented benchmarks under the same bit budget and the proposed framework further improves recognition accuracy under time-varying channels.

</details>


### [140] [Polynomially larger deletion codes by linear hashing of substring counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19493)
*Eyal En Gad*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文证明二进制纠删码可用约 (2t-1)log₂n 的冗余纠正 t 次删除；t=2 时达到 3log₂n+O(log₂log₂n)，改进此前系数 4 的最好上界。核心方法是随机线性哈希、冲突图分析与二染色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 删除信道中的码设计长期存在上下界差距：对纠正两次删除，已有最好上界的主导系数为4，而下界为2；一般 t 次删除时，上界系数为2t。论文旨在降低构造码的冗余，缩小上下界之间的差距，并改进长期未突破的两次删除结果。

Method: 首先对字符串的子串计数进行随机线性哈希，将所有词映射到约原构造所需标签数1/n的标签空间，并从一个标签类中提取候选码。随后构造冲突图：顶点表示具有相同标签且可能因删除而混淆的词，边表示不可同时保留的冲突关系。通过二染色处理可二分的冲突部分；对于含奇环的连通分量则先丢弃。关键分析表明，奇环会迫使环上编辑区域发生重叠，因此这类分量中的词数量很少。综合哈希筛选、奇环分量剔除和二染色，得到满足删除纠错要求的码。

Result: 对于纠正两次删除，存在冗余为3log₂n+O(log₂log₂n)的二进制码，相比此前最好上界4log₂n+O(log₂log₂n)降低了主导系数。更一般地，对任意t≥2，存在冗余为(2t-1)log₂n+O_t(log₂log₂n)的二进制码，将此前系数2t改进为2t-1。

Conclusion: 论文将多次删除纠错码的已知上界整体降低一个log₂n量级的主导项：两次删除情形取得3而非4的系数，一般情形取得2t-1而非2t。结果说明，基于子串统计的随机哈希结合冲突图结构分析，能够有效构造高效删除纠错码，并进一步缩小理论上下界差距。

Abstract: We show that binary codes of length $n$ correcting two deletions exist with redundancy $3\log_2n+O(\log_2\log_2n)$. The previous best upper bound had leading coefficient $4$, unchanged since 1965, while the best known lower bound has coefficient $2$. More generally, codes correcting $t\ge2$ deletions exist with redundancy $(2t-1)\log_2n+O_t(\log_2\log_2n)$, improving the coefficient $2t$. We extract a code from one label class of a random linear hash of substring counts, with about $n$ times fewer labels than a direct construction. Confusable words that still share a label are separated by a two-colouring after discarding the words in components with odd cycles, and these are few because an odd cycle forces the edits along it to overlap.

</details>


### [141] [A generalization of the map $χ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19548)
*Xiutao Feng,Qiang Wang,Jingyi Yu,Anpeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域 F_2^n 上一种循环移位不变的二次布尔映射，并将已知的 χ_n 映射推广，目标是完整刻画其中的所有置换映射。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 映射 χ_n 在轻量级密码学中具有重要应用，但对更一般的移位不变、代数次数为 2 的映射，其何时为置换仍缺乏完整描述。因此，论文旨在建立统一的结构化分类结果，为相关密码置换的设计与分析提供理论基础。

Method: 论文将 χ_n 推广到更一般的 F_2^n 上移位不变的二次映射，通过分析其坐标函数、循环结构以及置换条件，对满足条件的映射进行系统刻画。摘要未提供具体的代数工具、证明技术或分类参数。

Result: 论文声称完整刻画了所有移位不变且代数次数为 2 的置换映射，从而涵盖并推广了广泛研究的 χ_n 映射。摘要未给出具体分类形式、参数范围或构造数量。

Conclusion: 研究为轻量级密码学中循环对称二次置换的统一理解提供了完整理论描述，并扩展了 χ_n 映射的研究范围。

Abstract: The mapping $ χ_n:\mathbb{F}_2^n \to \mathbb{F}_2^n$ defined by $y=χ_n(x)$ with $y_i = x_i + x_{i+1}x_{i+2} + x_{i+2}$, where the indices are computed modulo $n$, has been widely studied for its application in lightweight cryptography. In this paper, we generalize this mapping and completely characterize all these shift-invariant permutations of algebraic degree $2$.

</details>


### [142] [Online Material-Labeled Environment Reconstruction via Bayesian Multipath Attribution for Low-Altitude ISAC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19819)
*Meihui Liu,Shu Sun,Ruifeng Gao,Qiuming Zhu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向低空一体化感知与通信（ISAC）的在线概率框架，将建筑立面表示为虚拟锚点（VA），联合完成立面定位、多径分量（MPC）归属和材料推断，从而生成材料标注的环境地图。仿真结果表明，该方法优于无归属基线，最终立面材料识别准确率达到93.75%，并保持较高的VA立面定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低空ISAC环境重建主要关注几何结构，忽略建筑材料对无线传播的影响，因而难以支持传播感知的信道预测和无人机网络优化。在多建筑室外场景中，不同立面产生的多径分量相互混杂，MPC到立面的对应关系不确定，同时无人机测量具有序列性且观测几何随时间变化，导致材料标注地图的构建具有较大挑战。

Method: 构建统一的在线概率推断框架：1）将每个反射立面建模为虚拟锚点，并通过贝叶斯方法估计立面级几何位置；2）利用包含镜面与漫反射效应的似然模型，计算MPC到各VA的软归属概率；3）根据归属概率构造归属感知的MPC表示，并以VA为中心聚合；4）通过材料推断网络生成立面级材料证据；5）采用在线贝叶斯更新递归融合多时刻证据，得到稳定的材料后验概率和材料标注环境地图。

Result: 在具有代表性的城市街道射线追踪仿真中，该方法显著优于无MPC归属的基线方法；在留出的无人机飞行轨迹上，最终立面级材料识别准确率达到93.75%，同时保持准确的基于VA的立面定位能力。

Conclusion: 该研究证明，将贝叶斯立面定位、概率化多径归属与在线材料推断进行联合建模，能够有效解决动态观测条件下的多建筑材料重建问题。所生成的材料标注地图比单纯几何地图更适合传播感知的环境建模、信道预测及低空无人机通信网络应用，但当前结论主要基于射线追踪仿真，仍需通过真实外场测量验证泛化能力。

Abstract: Environment reconstruction for low-altitude integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) has largely focused on geometry-centric maps, overlooking material-dependent propagation effects. Material-labeled reconstruction is therefore a key step toward propagation-aware mapping, enabling more physically grounded channel prediction and uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV) networking. However, constructing such maps from wireless multipath observations is challenging in outdoor multi-building scenarios because multipath components (MPCs) from different facades are mixed, path-to-facade attribution is uncertain, and UAV measurements arrive sequentially under time-varying observation geometries. To address these challenges, we propose a unified online probabilistic framework that represents each reflecting facade as a virtual anchor (VA) and couples Bayesian VA localization, multipath attribution, and material inference. The Bayesian front end estimates facade-level geometry and computes soft MPC-to-VA attribution probabilities using a speculardiffuse likelihood model, thereby accounting for both dominant specular paths and diffuse surface-interacted components. These attribution probabilities are used to construct attribution-aware MPC representations, which are aggregated in a VA-centric manner and mapped by a material inference network to facadelevel material evidence. The resulting evidence is recursively fused through an online Bayesian update to produce stable material posteriors and material-labeled environment maps. Ray-tracing simulations in a representative urban street scenario show that the proposed method substantially outperforms a no-attribution baseline, achieves 93.75% final facade-level material accuracy on a held-out UAV trajectory, and maintains accurate VA-based facade localization.

</details>


### [143] [Equivalence Between Nested Gibbs Measures and Log-Linear Combinations of Gibbs Measures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19988)
*Yaiza Bermudez,Samir M. Perlaza,Iñaki Esnaola*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究Gibbs概率测度的三类运算：幂归一化（重整化）、密度的归一化对数线性组合，以及参考测度替换意义下的嵌套。作者证明后两类运算所得测度均可表述为带相对熵正则项的优化问题，并识别出使嵌套与对数线性组合等价的一组参数。应用上，该等价性解释了一次性联邦学习中聚合本地Gibbs算法与基于全部本地数据集中训练的Gibbs算法为何能够达到相同性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gibbs概率测度广泛用于统计学习、变分推断和熵正则化优化，但不同测度运算之间的关系，尤其是对数线性组合与嵌套操作是否具有统一的优化解释，尚不清晰。本文旨在系统刻画这些运算的结构、区分各自对正则化因子与支持集的影响，并为联邦学习中的模型聚合提供理论依据。

Method: 首先分析对Gibbs密度进行幂归一化时，归一化因子变化与支持集收缩之间的作用机制，并说明二者可由不同参数独立调控。随后定义Gibbs密度的归一化对数线性组合，以及将一个Gibbs测度作为另一个测度参考测度的嵌套操作。通过变分优化与相对熵正则化分析，证明这两类操作产生的测度分别对应于目标函数线性组合的熵正则优化问题；进一步比较两类优化问题的系数，推导出使嵌套与对数线性组合结果一致的参数条件。最后将该理论应用于一次性联邦学习中的本地Gibbs算法聚合。

Result: 研究证明：1）幂归一化同时改变正则化因子并将支持集中到原支持集的子集，且这两种效应可独立控制；2）归一化对数线性组合和嵌套操作的结果仍是Gibbs概率测度；3）二者都等价于带相对熵正则化的期望优化问题，区别仅在于目标函数线性组合的系数；4）存在一组参数，使嵌套操作与对数线性组合完全等效；5）在一次性联邦学习示例中，服务器对本地训练得到的Gibbs算法进行对数线性组合，其性能与直接使用所有本地数据联合训练的Gibbs算法相同。

Conclusion: 本文建立了Gibbs概率测度三类运算的统一理论联系，特别揭示了嵌套与对数线性组合之间的参数化等价性。该结果表明，适当设计测度运算即可实现分布式数据集上的全局优化效果，为联邦学习中的通信高效模型聚合和熵正则化统计学习提供了理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, three operations on Gibbs probability measures are studied. The first operation, often referred to as renormalization, takes one Gibbs probability measure and generates a new Gibbs measure by normalizing a power of its density. This normalization has a twofold effect: it changes the regularization factor and concentrates the support within a subset of the original support. Interestingly, these effects can be independently controlled by different parameters. The second operation consists of a normalized log-linear combination of the densities of Gibbs probability measures. The third operation takes two Gibbs probability measures and changes the reference measure of the latter with the former. Hence, the former is said to be "nested" within the latter, yielding a new Gibbs probability measure. The resulting measures from the second and third operations are also Gibbs probability measures and are shown, respectively, to solve optimization problems involving the expectations of linear combinations of the objective functions of the given measures, subject to a relative entropy regularization. These optimization problems differ exclusively in the coefficients of the linear combinations. This leads to the conclusion that there exists a set of parameters for which nesting one Gibbs probability measure into another has the same effect as log-linearly combining them. These operations have relevant applications in statistical learning. As an example, a one-shot federated learning system in which clients send their locally trained Gibbs algorithms to the server for log-linear combination is shown to achieve the same performance as a Gibbs algorithm trained upon the aggregation of all local training datasets.

</details>


### [144] [Tri-Hybrid Beamforming Design for Large-Scale MIMO ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20092)
*Tianyu Fang,Mengyuan Ma,Markku Juntti,Inkyu Lee,Joonhyuk Kang,Nhan Thanh Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究基于三混合（tri-HBF）架构的集成感知与通信（ISAC）波束赋形设计。该架构结合动态超表面天线（DMA）与混合波束赋形，在通信速率、感知互信息和能效之间进行权衡。作者构建加权多目标优化问题，并提出具有闭式更新的低复杂度迭代算法。结果表明，相比传统混合MIMO和仅DMA方案，tri-HBF可显著提升能效，仅付出较小的通信速率和感知互信息损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模天线阵列中的传统混合MIMO系统通常具有较高的硬件成本和功耗，而DMA虽能降低成本，但可能限制系统性能。面向ISAC场景，需要同时优化通信吞吐量与感知能力，因此有必要研究兼顾能效、通信性能和感知性能的tri-HBF波束赋形设计。

Method: 建立以通信和感知性能为核心的加权多目标优化问题，目标联合提升通信和速率与感知和互信息；约束包括总发射功率以及tri-HBF架构的物理限制。针对该非凸问题，利用其结构设计低复杂度迭代算法，并通过闭式更新逐步求解各变量。

Result: 数值实验在多种配置下评估了所提架构。结果显示，tri-HBF相较传统混合MIMO和仅DMA配置具有显著更高的能效，同时通信和速率与感知和互信息仅出现较小程度的下降，体现出较好的性能—能耗折中。

Conclusion: tri-HBF架构适用于能效敏感的大规模ISAC系统，可在保持较高通信与感知性能的同时显著降低能耗。所提出的优化算法能够以较低复杂度处理相关非凸波束赋形设计问题，说明该架构具有潜在的工程应用价值。

Abstract: Tri-hybrid multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) architectures have been proposed as a promising solution for enabling energy-efficient communications systems in large-scale antenna arrays by replacing conventional antenna arrays in hybrid beamforming (HBF) systems with low-cost dynamic metasurface antennas (DMAs). In this work, we investigate beamforming design for integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) systems based on tri-HBF architectures, with the objective of jointly enhancing the communications sum rate and sensing sum mutual information. Specifically, we formulate a weighted multi-objective optimization problem that balances communications throughput and sensing mutual information, subject to a total transmit power consumption constraint and the physical limitations inherent to tri-HBF architectures. By exploiting the problem structure, we develop an efficient iterative algorithm with closed-form updates to solve the non-convex optimization problem with low complexity. Numerical results are provided to evaluate the performance of the proposed tri-HBF architecture in various setups. The results demonstrate that the proposed tri-HBF architecture can achieve significant improvement in energy efficiency (EE) compared to the conventional hybrid MIMO and DMA-only configurations, at the cost of minor degradations in sum rate and sensing mutual information.

</details>


### [145] [LEO Satellite Internet of Things: Architecture, Technology, and On-Orbit Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20165)
*Ming Ying,Xiaoming Chen,Qiao Qi,Yichao Xu,Jiajun Pan*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出面向6G物联网的低地球轨道（LEO）卫星系统二维架构，并围绕上行、下行和星间链路（ISL）分析三项关键使能技术：大规模免授权随机接入、基于深度学习的多波束预编码和分布式协作路由；同时通过在轨验证平台评估方案的实际可行性与性能，并总结开放问题及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星星座具备全球覆盖和泛在连接能力，有望成为6G物联网的重要基础设施。然而，卫星网络与地面网络的深度融合仍面临架构设计、随机接入、可靠传输、星间数据转发以及实际部署验证等系统性挑战，因此需要建立统一的系统架构并评估关键通信技术。

Method: 首先，从组成视角和功能视角构建6G LEO卫星物联网的二维系统架构，以支持LEO卫星与地面网络的无缝融合。随后分别针对上行、下行和星间链路研究三类技术：采用大规模免授权随机接入提升上行连接效率；利用深度学习开展多波束预编码以增强下行传输鲁棒性；采用分布式协作路由实现具有韧性的星间数据传输。最后搭建在轨验证平台，对所提出方案进行实际可行性和性能验证。

Result: 摘要表明，论文完成了统一系统架构设计，并对三项关键使能技术及其应用场景进行了系统分析；在轨验证平台进一步证明了相关方案具备现实部署可行性和一定性能优势。但摘要未给出具体实验指标、对比基线、数据集、提升幅度或统计显著性，因此无法定量判断各技术的具体收益。

Conclusion: LEO卫星物联网可通过架构、接入、传输和路由技术的协同设计，成为6G物联网的重要组成部分。本文为该方向提供了系统化技术框架和在轨验证基础，同时指出仍需围绕开放问题与未来研究方向持续推进，以实现大规模、可靠且高效的LEO卫星物联网。

Abstract: Low Earth orbit (LEO) satellite constellations are poised to become a cornerstone of the sixth-generation (6G) Internet of Things (IoT), providing truly global coverage and ubiquitous connectivity. This article presents a holistic two-dimensional system architecture for 6G LEO satellite IoT that incorporates composition and functional perspectives to facilitate the seamless integration of LEO satellites and terrestrial networks. Building upon this architecture, we evaluate three pivotal enabling technologies targeting the uplink, downlink, and inter-satellite links (ISLs). Specifically, we analyze massive grant-free random access for efficient uplink connectivity, investigate deep learning-based multibeam precoding for robust downlink transmission, and examine distributed cooperative routing for resilient ISL data delivery. Furthermore, we present an on-orbit verification platform that validates the real-world feasibility and performance of the proposed solutions. Finally, we outline key open challenges and future research directions to guide the realization of future LEO satellite IoT.

</details>


### [146] [Some MDS and ACD codes over commutative non-unital rings of orders 4 and 9 (Revision)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20190)
*Jon-Lark Kim,Marvin Olavides,Young Gun Roe*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限环 I_2 和 I_3 上的加性互补对偶码（ACD码），通过约化映射建立其与二元、三元 LCD 码的联系，并据此分类小长度下具有最佳距离的码；同时给出推广到 I_p 的一般关系，并修正先前论文中的若干定理及证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限环上的编码理论可产生具有良好距离和对偶性质的码，而 I_2、I_3 是典型的非交换性之外具有特殊幂零结构的交换非单位有限环。已有工作研究了这些环上的码，但 ACD 码的结构、最佳距离以及与经典有限域 LCD 码之间的联系仍需澄清。本文还旨在纠正先前发表版本中定理 3.5、4.7、4.8 及其证明的错误。

Method: 首先定义并研究 I_2 和 I_3 上的 ACD 码。利用从 I_2 到 F_2、从 I_3 到 F_3 的约化映射，将环上的 ACD 码与对应有限域上的二元或三元 LCD 码联系起来。借助这种对应关系，分析小长度码的最小汉明距离或最小 Lee 距离，分类达到最高距离的 ACD 码，并进一步将结论推广到 I_p 与 p 元 LCD 码之间的关系。

Result: 对于 I_2，本文分类了长度 n=1,2,3 时以及部分 n=4,5 情形下具有最高最小距离的 ACD 码，并证明这些码是 MDS 码。对于 I_3，分类了 n=1,2 时以及部分 n=3 情形下具有最高最小 Lee 距离的 ACD 码。文章还建立了 I_p 上 ACD 码与 F_p 上 p 元 LCD 码之间的一般化关系，并修正了此前版本中定理 3.5、4.7、4.8 及其证明。

Conclusion: 约化映射为研究 I_p 上的 ACD 码提供了有效途径，使许多问题能够转化为有限域 LCD 码问题。研究表明，在若干小长度情形下，I_2 上的最优 ACD 码具有 MDS 性质；I_3 情形也获得了相应的最优 Lee 距离分类。修正版结果增强了相关结论的准确性，并为更一般的 I_p 上 ACD 码研究奠定基础。

Abstract: There are eleven finite rings of order $p^{2}$ denoted by $A_p$ to $K_p$ in alphabetical order. In particular, we consider $I_{2}$ and $I_{3}$ which are commutative non-unital rings of orders 4 and 9 defined by generators and relations as \[I_{p}=\left\langle a,b\mid pa=pb=0,\:a^{2}=b,\:ab=0\right\rangle\] for $p=2, 3,$ respectively. Alahmadi et al. studied codes over these rings. In this paper, we study additive complementary dual (ACD) codes over the rings $I_{2}$ and $I_{3}$. We show relations between ACD codes over $I_{2}$ and binary linear complementary dual (LCD) codes using a reduction map from $I_{2}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{2}$, and between ACD codes over $I_{3}$ and ternary LCD codes using a reduction map from $I_{3}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{3}$. Using the first relation, we classify ACD codes over $I_{2}$ with the highest minimum distances for $n=1, 2, 3$ and partially for $n=4, 5$. It turns out that they are maximum distance separable (MDS) codes. Using the second relation, we classify ACD codes over $I_{3}$ with the highest minimum Lee distances for $n=1, 2$ and partially for $n=3$. We generalize the two relations into a relation between ACD codes over $I_{p}$ and $p$-ary LCD codes using a reduction map from $I_{p}$ to $\mathbb{F}_{p}$.
  This is a correction of the paper published in Advances in Mathematics of Communications, Volume 24, pages 61-76, 2026. In particular, we corrected the statements of Theorems 3.5, 4.7, 4.8, and their proofs.

</details>


### [147] [Conflict-Free Color-Clustered Sequential Belief-Propagation Decoding of Quantum LDPC Codes via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20236)
*Mohsen Moradi,Taejoon Kim,Remi A. Chou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无冲突的颜色聚类强化学习顺序BP译码器，通过将变量节点按冲突图着色，把原先逐变量节点的更新调度改为按颜色类别并行更新，在基本保持译码性能的同时显著降低调度决策开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子LDPC码的BP译码具有低复杂度和低时延优势，但短环、简并性及收敛失败会限制性能。基于强化学习的顺序BP译码能够根据综合征学习变量节点更新顺序并改善BP译码，但逐变量节点更新导致迭代内并行度不足、调度开销较大。因此，论文旨在提升RL顺序BP的并行性与调度效率，同时避免并行更新引发的消息冲突和短环干扰。

Method: 首先构建变量节点冲突图：若两个变量节点共享X型或Z型校验节点，则在图中连接。随后对冲突图进行着色，使同一颜色的变量节点具有互不重叠的校验节点邻域，从而保证它们可无冲突并行更新，并避免同一Tanner图4环或6环中的变量节点被同时更新。译码过程中，已训练的变量节点级Q表根据当前综合征选择一个种子变量节点，再将所有具有相同颜色的剩余变量节点组成批次，使用同一批预更新消息并行更新。

Result: 在去极化信道下的[[288,12,18]]双变量自行车码实验中，所提译码器的块错误率性能接近变量节点级RL顺序BP译码器。同时，每次BP迭代的调度决策数量从288个变量节点降低至11个颜色类别，显著减少了调度开销并提升了并行处理能力。摘要未提供具体错误率数值、复杂度增益或运行时间数据。

Conclusion: 颜色聚类能够在保留RL顺序BP译码性能的同时，将细粒度的逐变量节点调度转化为安全的批量并行调度。该方法为量子LDPC译码中兼顾性能、低延迟和硬件并行性的设计提供了有效方案，但其收益可能依赖于冲突图的色数、码结构及训练策略，仍需通过更多码型和信道条件验证泛化能力。

Abstract: Belief-propagation (BP) decoding for quantum low-density parity-check (QLDPC) codes is attractive due to its low complexity and low latency, but it is often limited by short cycles, degeneracy, and convergence failures. Reinforcement-learning-based sequential BP decoding (RL-S) improves BP by learning a syndrome-dependent variable-node (VN) update order, but its VN-by-VN schedule has limited within-iteration parallelism. In this paper, we propose a conflict-free color-clustered extension of RL-S. We construct a VN conflict graph in which two VNs are adjacent if they share an X-type or Z-type check, and color this graph so that same-color VNs have disjoint check neighborhoods. This also prevents VNs from the same Tanner 4- or 6-cycle from being updated simultaneously. During decoding, the trained VN-level Q-table selects a seed VN, and all remaining VNs with the same color are updated in parallel using the same pre-batch messages. For the [[288,12,18]] bivariate-bicycle code over the depolarizing channel, our proposed decoder achieves block-error-rate performance close to VN-level RL-S while reducing the scheduling decisions from 288 VNs to 11 color classes per BP iteration.

</details>


### [148] [A Mirror Vanishing Band for Weight Distributions of Binary Linear Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20344)
*Xianmang He*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 论文利用 Ashikhmin–Barg 关于最小向量的第三条性质，在二元线性码中建立位于 2d 另一侧的“镜像”权重消失区间，并改进非零权重数量上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有 Chen–Xie 结果利用 Ashikhmin–Barg 引理证明：当二元线性 [n,k,d] 码满足 k=n-2d+2+v 时，区间 [2d-v,2d-1] 内不存在码字重量。论文旨在进一步利用最小向量理论的第三条性质，在 2d 的另一侧构造对称的权重消失带，从而获得更强的非零重量数量上界。

Method: 利用 Ashikhmin–Barg 关于二元码非最小码字可分解为支集互不相交部分的性质。证明当 A_{d+1}=⋯=A_{d+t}=0（t≥1）且 k≥n-2d+1 时，所有 w∈[2d+1,2d+t] 都满足 A_w=0；随后将该镜像消失带与 Chen–Xie 已有的低于 2d 的消失带结合。

Result: 证明了区间 [2d+1,2d+t] 内不存在码字重量，并将此结果与已有区间 [2d-v,2d-1] 的消失性结合，使此类码的非零重量数至多为 n-d-v-2t+1，相比 Chen–Xie 的上界 n-d+1-v 改进了 2t。

Conclusion: 论文表明，二元线性码在最小距离之后若连续若干重量缺失，则这种缺失会通过非最小码字的支集分解性质在 2d 右侧产生镜像消失带。该结构性结果显著收紧了码的非零重量数量上界，说明局部重量谱空缺能够带来全局谱约束。

Abstract: Chen and Xie recently proved, using the Ashikhmin--Barg lemma on minimal vectors, that every binary linear $[n,k,d]$ code with $k=n-2d+2+v$ ($v\ge 0$) has no codewords of weight in the interval $[2d-v,\,2d-1]$. Their argument uses two of the five basic properties of minimal vectors established by Ashikhmin and Barg (1998). In this note we utilize the third property, the disjoint-support decomposition of non-minimal codewords in binary codes, to generate a \emph{mirror} vanishing band on the other side of $2d$: if $A_{d+1}=\cdots=A_{d+t}=0$ for some $t\ge 1$ and $k\ge n-2d+1$, then $A_w=0$ for all $w\in[2d+1,\,2d+t]$. Combining the two bands, the number of nonzero weights of such a code is at most $n-d-v-2t+1$, improving the Chen--Xie bound $n-d+1-v$ by $2t$.

</details>


### [149] [Norm-One Torus Decompositions and Decoding of Gashkov-Sidel'nikov Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.20402)
*Minjia Shi,Shitao Li,Yuhong Xia,Tor Helleseth,Ferruh Ozbudak*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究特征为3的有限域上Gashkov-Sidel'nikov循环码与准循环码的译码问题，刻画陪集重量、覆盖半径，并构造最大似然译码器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这类码的译码核心在于：给定综合，如何确定最小错误重量并构造达到该重量的错误向量。已有覆盖半径结果，但需要更完整的陪集重量分布、精确的最小错误重量判定以及对所有综合的显式译码构造。

Method: 令q=3^m、K=F_{q^2}，考虑范数为1的集合T。证明循环码和准循环码的带符号校验列标签恰为T，从而将译码转化为关于T的综合最小加法表示长度问题。进一步利用F_q上的范数与二次特征精确判定最小长度；通过二次特征和与Weil界，为陪集重量为3的每个综合构造对应的陪集首。

Result: 确定了任意综合的最小错误重量，并得到完整的陪集重量分布；重新证明覆盖半径为3。对于重量为3的情形，给出了可构造陪集首的方法，因此覆盖所有综合。

Conclusion: 该研究将两类Gashkov-Sidel'nikov码的译码问题统一为范数一集合上的加法长度问题，并结合有限域二次特征工具实现精确分类与构造性译码，最终获得完整的最大似然译码程序。

Abstract: Let $q=3^m$, let $K=\mathbb F_{q^2}$, and let \[\mathcal T=\{x\in K^*:\operatorname{N}_{K/\mathbb F_q}(x)=1\}.\] For both cyclic and constacyclic Gashkov-Sidel'nikov codes, we show that the set of signed parity-check column labels is precisely $\mathcal T$. Consequently, the decoding problem separates into two stages: determining the minimum error weight associated with a syndrome $S$ and constructing an error vector attaining this minimum. We identify the former quantity with the minimum additive length of $S$ with respect to $\mathcal T$ and determine it exactly by the norm and the quadratic character of $\mathbb F_q$. We also determine the complete coset-weight distribution and recover the known covering radius $3$. For the constructive part, we use quadratic-character sums and Weil bounds to construct a coset leader for every syndrome of coset weight three. The resulting procedures give complete maximum-likelihood decoders.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [150] [Regularized Emphatic Temporal-Difference Learning: Stability under Constant Stepsizes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19170)
*Xingguo Chen,Zhaohui Wu,Jinguo Ye,Chao Li,Shangdong Yang,Guang Yang,Skylar Liang,Wenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文指出：ETD虽能稳定其期望更新并改变投影几何，但不保证恒定步长采样动力学稳定；作者构造反例并提出RETD，通过后冲击修复改善采样稳定性，同时保持迹和重要性比不变，并恢复ETD固定点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ETD理论主要关注期望更新映射的收缩性与固定点性质，但这些性质未必能保证恒定步长下实际采样迭代的稳定性。论文旨在揭示二者之间的差异，分析不稳定来源，并提出一种不改变原有迹与重要性比的修复方法。

Method: 作者构造遍历两状态反例，利用随机矩阵乘积的最大Lyapunov指数和再生周期分析，区分采样动力学发散与后续迹无限方差的影响。在此基础上提出正则化强调TD（RETD）：将强调TD信号存入带泄漏的标量状态，并施加延迟校正；同时设计单正则化和双正则化读出，以恢复ETD固定点。理论上证明调和递减步长下的几乎处处收敛，并基于马尔可夫随机乘积界给出条件性的恒定步长矩收缩结果。实验采用成对的10,000次运行，在两状态构造和Baird反例上评估稳定性、固定点恢复及任务依赖性。

Result: ETD的均值映射可以收缩，但采样乘积的最大Lyapunov指数仍可能为正，说明均值稳定不等于采样稳定。RETD在两状态构造和一个Baird设置上获得经过证明的负Lyapunov指数；单正则化和双正则化读出能够精确恢复ETD固定点。实验验证了上述两种稳定性之间的分离、固定点恢复、非单调稳定区域以及任务依赖性。对于正Lyapunov指数的Baird ETD情形，结论仍主要是数值证据。

Conclusion: 论文表明，ETD的期望收缩性不能单独保证恒定步长采样过程稳定，稳定性还受随机乘积动力学和后冲击行为影响。RETD可在不改变共享的后续迹方差、迹或重要性比的前提下修复后冲击动力学，并兼顾稳定性与ETD固定点恢复；但它并不降低后续迹本身的方差，且其稳定效果具有任务依赖性。

Abstract: Emphatic temporal-difference learning (ETD) stabilizes the expected off-policy TD update and changes its projection geometry, but neither property determines constant-stepsize sampled dynamics. We construct an ergodic two-state counterexample in which the ETD mean map contracts while the sampled product has a positive top Lyapunov exponent. Regenerative-cycle analysis separates this sign from the infinite variance of the follow-on trace. We introduce regularized emphatic TD (RETD), a normalized first-order post-shock repair that leaves the trace and importance ratios unchanged, stores the emphatic TD signal in a leaky scalar state, and releases a delayed correction. RETD's raw equilibrium is an affine shift of the ETD equilibrium; single- and two-regularization readouts recover the ETD fixed point exactly. We prove almost-sure convergence for harmonic diminishing stepsizes and a conditional constant-stepsize moment-contraction result from a Markovian random-product bound. RETD has certified negative exponents on the two-state construction and one Baird point, whereas the positive Baird ETD sign remains numerical. Paired 10,000-run experiments validate both separations, fixed-point recovery, a nonmonotone stability region, and task dependence. RETD changes post-shock dynamics; it does not reduce the shared follow-on-trace variance.

</details>


### [151] [BioPhys-Bridge: A Benchmark for Interdisciplinary Scientific Reasoning in Physics-Grounded Biological Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19180)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 BioPhys-Bridge，这是一个面向生物物理学文献、强调证据溯源与定量科学推理的基准数据集。数据集包含 500 个案例、1,517 个任务，覆盖 6 个生物学领域和 9 类物理模型，并通过多项质量控制与专家审核。初步结果显示 DeepSeek-V4-Flash 的证据 ID F1 最高，但整体分数仍有限，说明复杂科学证据链推理仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言模型在跨学科科学文献分析中容易出现证据缺失、数值或单位错误、物理模型解释不充分，以及无法将观测结果连接到生物机制等问题。生物物理问题尤其要求同时完成文献证据定位、定量物理建模和生物学机制解释，因此需要专门的、可验证的评测基准。

Method: 作者构建 BioPhys-Bridge 数据集。每个案例包含证据块、稳定证据 ID、定量数值、单位、方程、假设、作用机制和后续决策等结构化目标，用于评测问答和检索增强生成。数据集初始版本包含 500 个案例与 1,517 个面向智能体的任务，覆盖 6 个生物学领域和 9 类物理模型，其中 3 类稀疏模型暂作为后续扩展方向。作者设置了模式规范、证据完整性、定量依据、来源许可、重复样本、单位标准化等质量门槛，并对 81 个案例进行领域专家审核和标注。

Result: 初步评测以证据 ID F1 为主要指标：DeepSeek-V4-Flash 得分最高，为 0.360；Qwen3.7-Max 为 0.316；GPT-4o-mini 为 0.294。结果表明模型在证据归因和定位方面仍存在明显不足，同时该基准能够支持对忠实性、幻觉抑制以及生物实验设计能力的评估。由于摘要仅报告单一或主要指标，尚不足以全面判断模型在定量推理、机制解释和决策质量上的表现。

Conclusion: BioPhys-Bridge 为生物物理文献中的证据 grounded 推理提供了专门的跨学科基准，能够系统评估模型是否依据原始证据完成定量分析并连接到生物机制。现有结果证明任务具有较高难度；未来需要扩大数据规模与复杂度，并开展更全面的模型、任务和泛化评估。

Abstract: Language models face unique challenges in analyzing interdisciplinary scientific research literature. In biophysics research, faithful answers require grounding observed data in source evidence, interpreting it through a quantitative physics model, and linking it to a biological mechanism. To address this challenge, we introduce BioPhys-Bridge, a novel benchmark dataset for evidence-grounded scientific reasoning over biophysical literature. Each case contains evidence blocks, stable evidence IDs, quantitative values, units, equations, assumptions, mechanisms, and next decisions as grounding targets for question answering (QA) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The initial release contains 500 cases, 1,517 agent-facing tasks, and covers six biological domains and nine physical model families, including three sparse families reserved for future expansion. We enforce strict quality gates for all cases in schema, evidence-integrity, quantitative-grounding, source-license, duplicate, unit-normalization, with domain expert review and annotation for 81 cases. Preliminary evaluations show that DeepSeek-V4-Flash obtain the highest evidence-ID $F_1$ score (0.360), followed by Qwen3.7-Max (0.316) and GPT-4o-mini (0.294). BioPhys-Bridge is an interdisciplinary benchmark for evaluating attribution, faithfulness, hallucination reduction, and biological experiment design with complex, multi-step scientific reasoning. Future works will increase the size and complexity of the dataset and perform comprehensive evaluations. Code and data are available in the GitHub repository and on Hugging Face.

</details>


### [152] [What Do We Expect from LLMs? Mapping the Design of LLM Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19182)
*Chao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统分析2022年1月至2026年8月间14,767篇引入或更新LLM评测资源的论文，考察评测对象、领域、材料、条件与评分机制的演变。研究发现，评测日益关注行动、交互和专业应用；LLM评分在智能体与非智能体评测中均增长，但模型生成评测材料未持续增加。文章同时警示，模型参与测试构造、任务执行和答案评判，可能削弱评测独立性并复制模型偏好与盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常以模型排名或基准分数衡量LLM进展，但较少系统考察“评测要求本身如何变化”。随着基准类型不断扩展，有必要分析研究者对LLM能力的期待、成功标准及模型参与评测流程的影响。

Method: 研究对象为arXiv在2022年1月至2026年8月提交的、引入或更新评测资源的14,767篇论文。作者采用分阶段筛选与自动化全文编码，系统分析目标系统和领域、评测材料与条件、评分机制，以及模型在测试构造、任务执行和评分中的参与情况。

Result: 评测重点逐渐转向行动、交互和专业应用，同时传统设计要素与新要素经常并存。模型参与呈现不均衡变化：基于LLM的评分在智能体和非智能体评测中都增加；相比之下，模型生成的评测材料在近期论文群体中没有显示出同等程度的持续增长。

Conclusion: LLM评测的发展体现了研究界如何将能力期待转化为具体测试和成功标准。评测规模扩大并不必然带来更独立的证据；当模型参与测试生成、任务完成和结果判定时，评测可能重现这些模型自身的偏好与盲点，因此需要进一步关注评测独立性与有效性。

Abstract: Benchmarks are central to how progress in large language models (LLMs) is assessed and communicated. Yet model rankings alone reveal little about how evaluation requirements themselves are changing. The expanding variety of benchmarks offers another perspective: what researchers expect LLMs to do, and what they count as successful performance. We systematically map 14,767 papers introducing or updating evaluation resources from arXiv submissions between January 2022 and August 2026. Using staged screening and automated full-text coding, we examine changes in target systems and domains, evaluation materials and conditions, and scoring mechanisms. The collection shows growing emphasis on action, interaction, and professional applications, while established and newer design elements frequently coexist. Model participation also develops unevenly: LLM-based scoring grows within both agent and non-agent groups, whereas model-generated materials show no comparable sustained increase in recent cohorts. These findings illuminate how public research translates capability expectations into concrete tests and criteria for success. As AI participates in constructing tests, performing tasks, and judging responses, they also raise a question: does expanding evaluation provide more independent evidence, or risk reproducing the preferences and blind spots of its participating models?

</details>


### [153] [Position: It is Time to Virtualize Foundation Models with a Self-evolving Operating System Layer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19203)
*Suparna Bhattacharya,Tarun Kumar,Cong Xu,Satish Kumar Mopur,Jiahao Li,Ashish Mishra,Aalap Tripathy,Annmary Justine Koomthanam,Martin Foltin,Ian Foster*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，提出“基础模型操作系统”（FMOS）作为复合智能体系统的统一系统层，通过虚拟化基础模型交互，统一管理状态、记忆、资源、验证与策略，从而提升可移植性、可信性和治理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工智能应用从单一基础模型转向由多个模型、工具和智能体组成的复合系统，现有框架虽然借助MCP、A2A等协议改善了连接性，但各自内置运行时，重复实现状态管理、记忆、预算控制和安全护栏，导致系统行为难以迁移、治理脆弱且生态碎片化。作者借鉴操作系统统一抽象硬件资源的思想，主张构建FMOS以解决这些基础设施层问题。

Method: 本文采用概念论证和系统架构设计，而非实证实验。核心方法是提出FMOS系统层：以虚拟机式抽象为应用提供近似独立且可信的基础模型实例；在内部通过多层记忆与知识编排、模型选择、资源分配、验证和策略执行来管理基础模型交互；同时利用类似人类快速直觉与慢速审慎思考的机制，学习何时介入推理、何时保持直接连续推理，并依据运行经验持续调整策略。

Result: 本文未报告定量实验结果或与基线系统的比较结果。其主要产出是一个研究命题和架构愿景：FMOS有望减少框架碎片化，提高应用和智能体行为的可移植性，统一资源与状态管理，并增强验证、策略执行和系统治理能力。

Conclusion: 作者认为，复合智能体系统的发展已经需要类似传统操作系统的统一系统层。FMOS可作为基础模型生态的通用运行时，通过虚拟化、资源编排、记忆管理和持续策略适应，为应用提供更强的能力抽象、可信性和治理基础。但由于本文属于立场论文，FMOS的具体接口、性能开销、策略学习机制及安全有效性仍需后续实现和实证验证。

Abstract: AI applications have shifted from single, monolithic foundation models (FM) to compound agentic systems. Yet today's stacks remain fragmented: even as protocols (e.g., MCP, A2A) ease tool/agent connectivity, each framework embeds an implicit runtime for state, memory, budgets, and guardrails, making behavior non-portable and governance brittle. It mirrors computing before operating systems, when every program re-implemented basic services. This position paper argues that the field now needs a Foundation Model Operating System (FMOS) -- a system layer that virtualizes FM interactions analogous to how virtual machines abstract physical hardware, giving applications the illusion of dedicated, trustworthy FM instances with effectively unbounded capabilities. Internally, the FMOS orchestrates knowledge across memory tiers, model selection and resource allocation, and verification and policy enforcement. Like the human brain switching between fast intuition and slow deliberation, the FMOS learns when to intervene and when to let inference proceed directly and continuously adapting its policies based on operational experience.

</details>


### [154] [What Do Current Systematic Generalization Tasks Miss? A Reasoning-Centered Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19212)
*Chengwen Qi,Deheng Ye,Yatao Bian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 TranSGrid，用统一任务同时考察演绎、归纳和溯因推理，以更严格地评估模型的系统泛化能力。对七个 Transformer、4800 个实例的实验表明，模型在 TranSGrid 上显著低于普通留出测试集；削弱归纳或溯因需求的任一简化都会使性能恢复到测试集水平，说明现有基准可能低估了系统泛化的真实难度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统泛化要求模型将已知的原子元素重新组合，以解决新问题，是人类智能的重要能力。但现有研究通常采用近似线性的动作组合、基于生产率的测试或动作显式目标等简化设定，这些设定降低了评估难度，无法充分考察归纳和溯因等关键推理需求。因此，本文旨在构建一个更全面、受控且能区分不同推理需求的系统泛化测试环境。

Method: 作者提出 TranSGrid，将演绎推理、归纳推理和溯因推理整合到同一任务中。研究在4800个 TranSGrid 实例上评估七个 Transformer，并将其表现与留出测试集进行比较。此外，作者构造两个变体：其一使动作近似线性组合，以降低归纳需求；其二使目标显式包含动作信息，以降低溯因需求。通过比较原始任务与两个变体，分析不同简化对系统泛化评估的影响。

Result: 最大模型在普通留出测试集上的准确率为79.6%，在完整 TranSGrid 上降至55.3%，在最困难子集上仅为15.8%。即使测试长度处于训练长度范围内，性能差距仍然存在，说明仅依靠生产率或长度外推不足以衡量系统泛化。两个简化变体的解题率均恢复到大致接近留出测试集的水平，表明仅削弱归纳需求或溯因需求中的任一项，就足以显著降低任务难度。

Conclusion: 现有系统泛化任务往往通过降低归纳需求、溯因需求，或同时降低二者，将问题简化为普通的留出测试。要全面测量系统泛化能力，测试任务必须同时包含演绎、归纳和溯因三类推理需求。TranSGrid 为此提供了一个更严格的统一评估框架。

Abstract: Systematic generalization, the ability to solve novel problems by recombining known atomic elements, is central to human intelligence but difficult to study rigorously under controlled settings. Existing studies therefore rely on simplifications such as approximately linear action composition, productivity-based tests, and action-explicit goals, which make systematic generalization easier to study but omit some essential aspects of this capability. To characterize what these simplifications miss, we adopt a reasoning-centered lens and introduce TranSGrid, a testbed that brings deductive, inductive, and abductive reasoning together within a unified task. Experiments with seven Transformers on 4,800 TranSGrid instances show that all models perform much worse on TranSGrid than on a held-out test set: the largest model solves 79.6% of the test set, but only 55.3% of TranSGrid and 15.8% of the hardest subset. The gap remains within the training length range, showing that productivity alone is not sufficient to evaluate systematic generalization. Additionally, we reintroduce the other two simplifications into TranSGrid: one variant makes actions compose almost linearly (reducing the inductive demand), the other makes goals action-explicit (reducing the abductive one). In both, solve rates return to roughly the test set level, showing that either simplification alone is enough to reduce TranSGrid to an ordinary held-out test set. Together, our results show that existing tasks reduce either or both of the inductive and abductive demands, and that comprehensively measuring systematic generalization requires a task that involves all three forms of reasoning.

</details>


### [155] [Characterizing Web Search by Conversational LLM Agents: From Search Decisions and Strategies to Results and Responses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19244)
*Mahsa Amani,Seungeon Lee,Abhisek Dash,Asmaa El Fraihi,Yunah Jang,Elisabeth Kirsten,Qinyuan Wu,Krishna P. Gummadi,Manish Gupta,Abhilasha Ravichander,Muhammad Bilal Zafar,Soumi Das*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究系统分析四个主流对话式平台中的智能体网络搜索生命周期，比较其搜索触发、查询构造、结果来源偏好和基于搜索结果生成回答的行为。研究发现，不同平台的搜索决策和查询策略差异显著；更频繁搜索不一定带来更高回答质量；搜索结果存在平台偏好的来源域；回答虽大体基于搜索结果，但部分论断未得到明确引用，影响可追溯性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着对话式大语言模型越来越依赖网络搜索，智能体从决定是否搜索、如何构造查询，到如何利用搜索结果生成回答的完整流程仍缺乏系统理解。现有研究难以同时揭示真实用户交互与受控模型行为之间的差异，也不足以评估搜索频率、查询策略、来源偏好和引用归因对回答质量与可靠性的影响。因此，该研究旨在填补对话式智能体网络搜索端到端生命周期研究的空白，并为未来智能体和对话式检索工具的设计提供依据。

Method: 研究覆盖ChatGPT、Claude、Grok和DeepSeek四个对话式平台，结合两类实验设置：一是基于真实用户交互的invivo分析，二是通过各平台模型API开展、使用相同平台模型的受控invitro实验。研究重点考察四个环节：是否调用网络搜索的决策质量、搜索查询的构造策略、搜索结果中的域名或来源偏好，以及模型将搜索结果转化为有依据回答时的选择与引用情况。

Result: 研究发现：1）不同平台和模型在是否调用网络搜索方面存在显著差异；2）更高的网络搜索调用频率并不必然带来更高的回答质量；3）对话式智能体采用多样且复杂的查询构造策略；4）平台特定的搜索引擎会返回来自其偏好域名的结果，表明可能存在来源分布偏差；5）生成的回答总体上能够基于搜索结果，但部分论断依赖未被引用的搜索结果，导致归因不完整，并对回答的可验证性和可靠性构成风险。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，对话式智能体的网络搜索能力不能仅通过搜索次数或最终回答质量进行评价，而应从搜索触发、查询生成、来源分布和证据归因等多个环节进行端到端评估。未来系统应优化搜索决策与查询策略，降低来源偏好带来的偏差，并强化逐项论断的证据链接和引用机制，以提升对话式检索的透明度、可追溯性和可靠性。

Abstract: Conversational LLM agents increasingly rely on Web search, yet the end-to-end lifecycle of agentic search remains poorly understood. We present the first study of Web search across four major conversational platforms (ChatGPT, Claude, Grok, and DeepSeek), combining real-world user interactions (invivo) with controlled experiments using the same platform's models by their APIs (invitro). We investigate the quality of agentic decisions to invoke Web search, their strategies to formulate queries, the potential domain preferences in the search results they receive, and the choices they make when transforming search results into grounded responses. We find that Web-search decisions vary substantially across platforms and models, while more frequent Web-search invocation does not necessarily yield better response quality. We further show that conversational agents employ different complex querying strategies and that platform specific search engines return search results from their preferred domains. Finally, although responses are largely grounded in search results, some claims rely on uncited search results, raising concerns about attribution and reliability. Our findings have important implications for the design of future AI agents and Web search tools optimized for conversational retrieval.

</details>


### [156] [Do AI Agents Understand Computer Architecture?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19387)
*Ambika Sharan,Grigory Chirkov,Soheil Abbasloo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 AutoTuring，通过在相同智能体、相同15维加速器搜索空间、相同评估器和最优解条件下，对比“具名架构参数+模拟器计数器”与“匿名[0,1]变量”两种问题表述，测量架构语义是否真正帮助硬件设计。初步结果显示，具备架构语义的智能体平均优于盲搜索，并显著减少模拟器调用；但结构化批评循环可让盲智能体追回大部分差距，说明架构知识与结构化批评可能是替代关系而非互补关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体硬件设计研究通常只证明性能得到提升，却无法判断智能体是否理解硬件机器，还是仅仅在不理解参数含义的情况下高效搜索。由于既有评估固定了问题表述，无法区分真正的架构推理与通用搜索能力。本文的动机是构造一个能够隔离“问题是否具有语义”的公平对照实验，并检验架构知识的可迁移价值。

Method: 提出 AutoTuring 实验框架。对同一个智能体使用同一个15维加速器设计空间，分别提供两种表述：一是具名架构旋钮及模拟器计数器，二是匿名的[0,1]变量。保持评估器、合法设计空间和可达最优解完全一致，仅改变问题是否具有架构语义。实验在九个FP16 GEMM内核组成的测试集上进行，并进一步加入批评循环，比较架构知识与结构化批评的作用。

Result: 具名架构表述的智能体相较于建模的H200平均提升5.4%，相较于匿名盲智能体平均提升12.3%，同时模拟器调用次数减少70.1%。不过，加入批评循环后，匿名智能体可追回大部分性能差距，而该机制对具名架构智能体没有额外收益，表明架构知识与结构化批评可能具有替代关系。结果基于单个建模加速器、每种条件5至6次运行，因此应视为初步发现。

Conclusion: 问题表述中的架构语义本身能够提升硬件设计智能体的性能与搜索效率，但这种优势并非不可替代；高质量的结构化批评也能提供相近收益。本文的主要贡献不是提出一个更强的加速器，而是提出并展示一种区分“理解架构”与“无语义高效搜索”的对照测量方法。由于实验规模有限，结论仍需在更多架构、任务和重复实验中验证。

Abstract: Agents are increasingly asked to design hardware, and increasingly reported to succeed. Such reports establish that a design improved; they cannot establish why. An agent that improves an accelerator may be reasoning about the machine, or may be searching competently over knobs whose meaning it never recovers -- and only the first transfers to the next architecture. Existing evaluations cannot tell the two apart, because they vary the agent while holding the framing of the problem fixed. We do the opposite. AutoTuring hands the same agent the same 15-dimensional accelerator space twice: once as named architectural knobs with simulator counters, once as anonymous variables on [0,1], with the evaluator, the legal space and the reachable optima held identical, so that the only thing that varies is whether the problem means anything. The gap between the two is the measurement. On a nine-kernel FP16 GEMM basket, meaning pays: the architect beats a modeled H200 by 5.4% and its blind counterpart by 12.3% on average, with 70.1% fewer simulator calls. It does not pay uniquely: a critic loop recovers most of that gap for the blind agent and buys the architect nothing, so architectural knowledge and structured critique behave as substitutes rather than as complements. We report these as preliminary findings -- five to six runs per condition on a single modeled accelerator -- and take the comparison itself, not the accelerator, to be the contribution.

</details>


### [157] [MAGS: Multi-agent Auto-formalization Guarantees Safety for Agentic Outputs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19391)
*Albert Wu,Nicholas Roberts,Tzu-Heng Huang,Haoran Lin,Gil Friedman,Sungjun Cho,Gabriel Orlanski,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MAGS 是一个面向 LLM 编程代理的多智能体形式化验证框架：将生成代码转译为 Dafny，依据冻结且经人工审计的 API 与安全规范进行机械化检查和自动修复，再编译回可执行程序。该方法在 220 个 CUDA、终端脚本和机械臂任务上均成功生成满足非平凡安全保证的程序，但其可靠性仍取决于形式化语义是否完整刻画目标行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 编程代理能够生成复杂程序，使人工全面审查变得困难，从而增加安全与安保故障风险。模糊测试、静态分析和 LLM-as-a-Verifier 等方法虽能发现部分问题，却难以覆盖所有边界情况。传统形式化验证可以提供机器可检查的保证，但通常需要大量人工规格编写和证明工程，因此论文旨在降低这一门槛，并为生成程序提供可验证的安全保证。

Method: 提出统一的多智能体框架 MAGS。首先冻结经过人工审计的 API 和安全需求；随后将 LLM 生成的代码转换为具备验证意识的中间表示 Dafny；利用 Dafny 验证器机械检查安全属性，并根据验证器反馈修复违规代码；最后将验证通过的程序重新编译为可执行代码。该流程结合了代码生成、自动形式化、验证反馈和程序修复。

Result: 在 100 个 CUDA 内核、100 个终端脚本和 20 个机械臂任务组成的 220 个样例上，MAGS 达到 100% 的成功率，均生成了满足冻结规范下非平凡安全保证的程序。独立的安全性和功能性评估显示，框架在三个领域整体表现较强；但实验也发现，当自动形式化得到的语义不能完整表达目标行为时，系统仍会失败。

Conclusion: MAGS 表明，将 LLM 代码生成与 Dafny 形式化验证、验证反馈修复相结合，能够在多个程序领域稳定地产生带机器可检查安全保证的代码。其核心优势是把部分人工证明工程转化为自动化流程。然而，100% 的结果主要针对冻结的形式化规范，并不等同于真实环境中的绝对安全；系统最终保证的有效性取决于 API、需求和自动形式化语义的准确性与完备性。

Abstract: LLM coding agents now generate complex programs at a scale that makes thorough human review increasingly difficult, raising the risk of safety and security failures. Common approaches, including fuzz testing, static analysis, and LLM-as-a-Verifier, can detect many failures but struggle to cover all possible edge cases. Formal verification addresses this by providing machine-checkable guarantees over specified properties, but traditionally demands substantial manual specification and proof engineering. We introduce a unified multi-agent framework, MAGS, that generates executable programs with formal safety guarantees, using Dafny as a verification-aware intermediate representation where safety properties can be mechanically checked. MAGS formalizes and freezes human-audited APIs and safety requirements, translates generated code into Dafny, repairs violations using verifier feedback, and compiles verified programs back into executable code. We evaluate MAGS on 100 CUDA kernels, 100 terminal scripts, and 20 robotic-arm tasks. Across all 220 examples, it achieves a 100% success rate in producing programs with non-trivial safety guarantees against frozen specifications. Independent safety and functional evaluations further show strong performance across all three domains, while revealing failures when the auto-formalized semantics do not fully capture the target behavior.

</details>


### [158] [Closed-World Resolution Against Tool Hallucination in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19425)
*Laxmipriya Ganesh Iyer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究工具增强型大语言模型代理的工具幻觉问题，提出五类幻觉分类、训练无关的闭世界解析器与HTB基准，并通过单注册表和MCP多服务器场景的实证测量证明，幻觉检测必须先于工具选择或安全门控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具选择和工具安全防御都默认模型生成的调用指向真实工具且参数符合工具接口，因此无法处理不存在的工具调用或未被任何 schema 声明的参数。论文旨在揭示这一结构性盲点，系统测量工具幻觉，明确防御链路中的必要位置，并分析多服务器合并场景带来的新型风险。

Method: 论文首先提出工具幻觉五分类体系（H1-H5），并设计训练无关、闭世界的 Resolution Rung，通过工具注册表成员关系和签名检查判断调用是否合法。随后在十个托管模型、两种调用界面上进行基准测量，比较受约束接口与原始 JSON 接口下的幻觉表现。进一步将分析扩展到 Model Context Protocol（MCP）多服务器合并命名空间，提出第二套 M1-M5 分类，研究工具名称冲突与 shadowing 对解析的影响，并发布版本化 Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark（HTB）。理论上，论文证明幻觉防御必须位于任何因果工具门控之前，同时指出无法消除的残余类型：借用的参数在 schema 层面与合法调用不可区分。

Result: 在单注册表场景中，十个模型共观察到322次真实工具幻觉；虚构工具调用主要集中在约束较弱的原始 JSON 接口，数量为34次，而另一种调用界面仅为3次。模型规模并未带来明显改善，675B模型的表现与7—8B模型相当。在MCP实时多服务器场景中观察到154次幻觉，甚至包括在单注册表场景中表现正常的前沿模型；原因在于服务器合并产生的工具名冲突和 shadowing 是单一注册表难以表达的结构性问题。

Conclusion: 工具幻觉不是普通的工具选择错误，也不是现有安全门控能够覆盖的问题，而是必须在工具选择与策略门控之前单独处理的解析层问题。闭世界注册表与签名检查可作为可比较的基线，但对 schema indistinguishable 的借用参数存在不可约残余。MCP 的多服务器合并进一步扩大了攻击面和测量空间。HTB为后续解析器和防御方法提供了统一评测基础。

Abstract: Tool-augmented large language model (LLM) agents fail in a way no tool-selection or tool-security method addresses: they call tools that do not exist and pass arguments no schema declares. Existing defenses either pick the right tool (selection) or constrain what an agent may do with real tools (gating), both of which presuppose the emitted call refers to a real tool at all. We show this is a structural blind spot: a hallucinated call is by construction not a decision any gate made, so no gate can reject it. This paper is primarily a measurement and benchmark study. We give a five-class taxonomy of tool hallucination (H1-H5) and, as a reference point, the Resolution Rung: a training-free, closed-world resolver (registry membership plus a signature check) whose interest is where it must sit, not what it computes. We prove hallucination defense must precede any causal gate, and characterize the one irreducible residue (borrowed arguments schema-indistinguishable from a valid call). Across ten hosted models under two invocation surfaces we measure 322 genuine hallucinations; fabricated-tool calls concentrate on the unconstrained raw-JSON surface (34 vs. 3), and model scale does not help (a 675B model matches a 7-8B one). We then extend to the Model Context Protocol, where merging several servers into one namespace creates hallucination surfaces a single registry cannot express (a second taxonomy, M1-M5); on the live MCP surface we measure 154 hallucinations, including from frontier models that were clean on the single-registry surface, because collisions and shadowing are structural to the merge. We release the versioned Hallucinated-Tools Benchmark (HTB) so any resolver is comparable across submissions.

</details>


### [159] [The syntax and semantics of goals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19448)
*David M. Abel,Mark K. Ho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于“目标表示”的综述性/理论性论文，主张将目标视为具有形式与意义的组合式认知表征，并据此分析其表达能力、行为约束、等价性与设计空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有认知科学与计算机科学通常把目标视为固定的行为约束，但较少系统区分目标的表示形式与语义内容。作者借鉴语言学和逻辑学中的句法—语义接口，试图回答：不同目标语言能表达哪些行为、不同表示是否可能对应相同的行为，以及目标表示本身受到哪些约束。

Method: 本文采用概念综合与理论分析方法，而非提出单一实验或计算模型。作者整合认知科学、计算机科学、语言学和逻辑学中关于目标表示的研究，将目标表示的属性组织为一个更广泛的设计空间，并从形式（representation/form）与意义（content/meaning）的区分出发分析其表达能力、效率、约束和行为等价性。

Result: 论文指出，目标是能够与世界知识灵活组合、组织有目的行为的组合式表征。不同目标表示在表达能力、效率和可刻画的行为范围上存在差异；某些形式不同的目标表示可能产生相同或等价的行为。区分目标的形式与意义，还能揭示关于目标的隐含假设，并为比较不同目标观念提供明确的变化维度。

Conclusion: 目标不应仅被理解为对行为施加固定约束的状态，还应被研究为具有句法结构和语义内容的表征系统。分析目标表示的设计空间，有助于连接高级认知与动机研究，澄清目标的表达性与实现方式，并更精确地比较不同理论中的目标概念。

Abstract: In both cognitive science and computer science, goals are conceptualized as cognitive states that flexibly combine with world knowledge to organize and specify purposeful behavior. In this way, goals are compositional representations whose content relates to rational behavior. We here draw attention to goals as representations and their content because it highlights a parallel with other areas in cognitive science - in particular, the syntax-semantics interface in linguistics and logic - while also foregrounding foundational questions about the expressivity, design, and efficiency of different goal representations. For example, goals are typically taken as fixed and imposing constraints on desirable behaviors, but we can also identify constraints on goal representations themselves, such as whether a particular goal language is sufficiently expressive to capture behaviors of interest, or whether different goal representations capture the same behavior. Here, we synthesize work that aims to characterize the properties of different goal representations and suggest these are points of a broader design space. We close by discussing how distinguishing the form and meaning of goals can elucidate the implicit assumptions we make about goals, inform the study of interactions between higher-level cognition and motivation, and isolate axes of variation for different conceptions of goals.

</details>


### [160] [Compositional Reasoning in Language Models under Reinforcement Learning Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19465)
*Yu He,Yingxi Li,Yifei Wang,Ellen Vitercik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究强化学习等后训练方法对语言模型组合推理能力的影响，提出依赖图框架定义三层组合性，并通过数据结构任务发现“分解到组合”的迁移不对称：在分解任务上训练难以稳定迁移到组合任务，而在组合任务上训练更容易迁移回分解任务；该现象还在长度外推、结构分布偏移、未见技能迁移及初步工具调用任务中得到考察。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实问题求解通常要求模型将已学技能以新方式组合，而有限训练数据使组合泛化成为关键能力。尽管强化学习等后训练方法提升了语言模型的推理表现，但其对组合推理的作用及迁移规律仍缺乏系统理解。本文旨在形式化组合推理、刻画不同复杂度层级，并解释分解任务与组合任务之间为何存在不对称迁移。

Method: 作者提出基于依赖图的组合推理框架，将组合性划分为三个复杂度递增的层级。实验以数据结构任务为主要载体，因为这类任务具有确定性的奖励计算方式和清晰的组合结构。研究比较分解技能训练与组合任务训练之间的迁移效果，并进一步测试长度外推、结构分布偏移以及向需要未见技能的任务迁移。最后，在真实世界工具调用基准上开展初步验证，以考察结论的实际适用性。论文还给出了关于迁移不对称性的理论解释。

Result: 实验 consistently 发现分解到组合的迁移不对称：仅在分解技能上训练，通常不能可靠提升组合任务表现；相反，在组合任务上训练后，模型更容易迁移回分解任务。该规律在长度外推、结构分布变化和未见技能迁移等设置下得到进一步评估，并在工具调用任务中获得初步支持。不过摘要未报告具体数据集、模型规模、提升幅度或统计显著性。

Conclusion: 后训练，尤其是强化学习，对组合推理的收益取决于训练任务的结构。直接训练组合任务可能比仅训练其分解子技能更有效，因为组合训练能够同时塑造技能及其依赖关系、执行顺序和交互方式。本文的依赖图框架为研究组合泛化提供了统一视角，但工具调用部分仍属初步结果，结论的跨领域普适性还需要更多任务、模型和更严格的对照实验验证。

Abstract: Compositional reasoning is critical for real-world problem solving: since training data is necessarily limited, models must generalize by composing learned skills in new ways. While post-training methods such as reinforcement learning (RL) have substantially improved the reasoning abilities of language models (LMs), their effects on compositional reasoning remain less well understood. We propose a dependency-graph framework to formalize compositional reasoning, yielding three levels of compositionality with increasing complexity. Empirically, we instantiate this framework with data-structure tasks, which provide deterministic reward computation and clear compositional structure. We find a consistent decomposed-to-composed asymmetry: decomposed-skill training does not reliably transfer to composed tasks, whereas composed-task training transfers more readily back to decomposed tasks. We provide theoretical explanation for this asymmetry, and further evaluate compositional generalization under length extrapolation, structural distribution shift, and transfer to tasks requiring unseen skills. Finally, we present a pilot study on real-world tool-calling benchmarks, showing preliminary evidence that the decomposed-to-composed asymmetry can extend to practical settings.

</details>


### [161] [Safety Beyond the Interface: Detecting Harm via Latent States in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.19472)
*Alizishaan Khatri,Chiquita Prabhu,Omkar Neogi*

Main category: cs.AI
